> DEVELOPER

Look at the local git diff (unstaged changes) in this repo. Focus on understanding the feature where, when the work queue reaches the end of a batch and there are subjective items that need review or are stale, the system updates them or adds them to the next queue.

Key files to examine via `git diff`:
1. `desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py` and `ranking.py` — work queue logic
2. `desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` — stale dimension detection
3. `desloppify/app/commands/next.py` and `next_parts/output.py` and `next_parts/render.py` — the next command
4. `desloppify/engine/_scoring/detection.py` — scoring detection
5. `desloppify/engine/_state/scoring.py` and `filtering.py` — state scoring/filtering

Read the diffs carefully and summarize:
- What the new/changed logic does
- How it detects "end of batch" or "all items done"
- How it identifies subjective/stale items
- How it queues them for refresh
- The overall flow

Use `git diff` commands to read the changes. You can also read full files if context is needed.

> AGENT

I'll start by reading the git diffs for all the key files mentioned.

+def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
+    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
+    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
+        return any(
+            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
+            for m in item.get("members", [])
+        )
+    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
+
+
+def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
+    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
+    try:
+        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
+        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
+        if meta:
+            return meta.action_type
+    except ImportError:
+        pass
+    return "manual_fix"
+
+
+_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
+
+
+def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
+    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
+    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
+    if not clusters:
+        return items
+
+    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
+    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
+    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
+        if not cluster.get("auto"):
+            continue
+        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
+            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
+
+    if not fid_to_cluster:
+        return items
+
+    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
+    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
+    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
+
+    for item in items:
+        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
+        if cname:
+            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
+        else:
+            non_cluster_items.append(item)
+
+    # Build cluster meta-items
+    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
+    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
+        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
+        if len(members) < 2:
+            result.extend(members)
+            continue
+        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
+        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
+        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
+        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
+        if "desloppify fix" in action:
+            action_type = "auto_fix"
+        elif "desloppify move" in action:
+            action_type = "reorganize"
+        else:
+            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
+            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
+            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
+                action_type = "refactor"
+
+        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
+        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
+        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
+        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
+            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
+            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
+        else:
+            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
+
+        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
+        if not primary_command:
+            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
+
+        meta_item: dict = {
+            "id": cname,
+            "kind": "cluster",
+            "action_type": action_type,
+            "summary": summary,
+            "members": members,
+            "member_count": len(members),
+            "primary_command": primary_command,
+            "cluster_name": cname,
+            "cluster_auto": True,
+            "confidence": "high",
+            "detector": detector,
+            "file": "",
+        }
+        result.append(meta_item)
+
+    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
+    return result
+

 def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
+    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
+        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
+        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
+        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
+        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
+            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
+        )
+        return (
+            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
+            action_pri,
+            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
+            item.get("id", ""),
+        )
+
     if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
         return (
             int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
@@ -156,6 +173,12 @@ def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
 def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
     counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
     for item in items:
+        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
+            # Count each member at its own tier
+            for member in item.get("members", []):
+                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
+                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
+            continue
         tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
         counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
     return counts

+_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {

+def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
+    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
+    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
+    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
+    type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
+    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
+    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
+    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
+
+    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
+    members = item.get("members", [])
+    if members:
+        # File distribution
+        from collections import Counter
+        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
+        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
+            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
+            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
+                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
+        else:
+            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
+            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
+                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
+            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
+            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
+
+        # Sample IDs
+        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
+        for m in members[:3]:
+            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
+        if len(members) > 3:
+            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
+
+    # Action
+    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
+    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
+    if primary_command:
+        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
+
+    # Resolution hints
+    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify resolve fixed "{cluster_name}" '
+                   '--note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
+    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
+                   "dim"))
+    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))

> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1→"""Sync stale subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Handles both directions:
     4→  1. **Cleanup** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale.
     5→  2. **Inject**  — when no objective items remain after cleanup, add stale dimensions.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→
    15→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    20→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    21→
    22→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→
    25→    @property
    26→    def changes(self) -> int:
    27→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    32→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    33→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    34→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    38→    if not dim_scores:
    39→        return set()
    40→
    41→    stale: set[str] = set()
    42→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    43→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    44→            continue
    45→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    46→        if dim_key:
    47→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    48→    return stale
    49→
    50→
    51→def sync_stale_dimensions(
    52→    plan: PlanModel,
    53→    state: StateModel,
    54→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    55→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
    56→
    57→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
    58→       longer stale (the user refreshed them, or mechanical evidence changed).
    59→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
    60→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    61→    """
    62→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    63→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    64→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    65→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    66→
    67→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    68→    to_remove: list[str] = [
    69→        fid for fid in order
    70→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in stale_ids
    71→    ]
    72→    for fid in to_remove:
    73→        order.remove(fid)
    74→        result.pruned.append(fid)
    75→
    76→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
    77→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
    78→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
    79→        existing = set(order)
    80→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
    81→            if sid not in existing:
    82→                order.append(sid)
    83→                result.injected.append(sid)
    84→
    85→    return result
    86→
    87→
    88→__all__ = [
    89→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    90→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    91→]
    92→
```

index 606450e..4f02485 100644
--- a/desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions.py
+++ b/desloppify/tests/plan/test_stale_dimensions.py
@@ -175,3 +175,108 @@ def test_reconcile_ignores_synthetic_ids():
     assert result.superseded == []
     assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]
     assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in plan.get("superseded", {})
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Injection: only subjective items in queue (relaxed condition)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_injection_when_only_subjective_in_queue():
+    """New stale dims are injected when only subjective IDs remain in queue."""
+    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")
+    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
+
+    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
+    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in result.injected
+    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in result.injected  # already there
+    assert set(plan["queue_order"]) == {
+        "subjective::design_coherence",
+        "subjective::error_consistency",
+    }
+
+
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Auto-clustering of stale subjective dimensions
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_stale_cluster_created():
+    """When >=2 stale dims exist, auto_cluster_findings creates a cluster."""
+    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
+
+    plan = _plan_with_queue(
+        "subjective::design_coherence",
+        "subjective::error_consistency",
+    )
+    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
+
+    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
+    assert changes >= 1
+    assert "auto/stale-review" in plan["clusters"]
+
+    cluster = plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]
+    assert cluster["auto"] is True
+    assert cluster["cluster_key"] == "subjective::stale"
+    assert set(cluster["finding_ids"]) == {
+        "subjective::design_coherence",
+        "subjective::error_consistency",
+    }
+    assert "Re-review 2 stale" in cluster["description"]
+    assert "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions" in cluster["action"]
+    assert "design_coherence" in cluster["action"]
+    assert "error_consistency" in cluster["action"]
+
+
+def test_stale_cluster_deleted_when_fresh():
+    """When all dims are refreshed, the stale cluster is deleted."""
+    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
+
+    plan = _plan_with_queue(
+        "subjective::design_coherence",
+        "subjective::error_consistency",
+    )
+    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
+
+    # Create the cluster
+    auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
+    assert "auto/stale-review" in plan["clusters"]
+
+    # Now remove subjective IDs from queue (simulating refresh + prune)
+    plan["queue_order"] = []
+
+    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
+    assert "auto/stale-review" not in plan["clusters"]
+
+
+def test_stale_cluster_updated():
+    """When the stale set changes, the cluster membership updates."""
+    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
+
+    plan = _plan_with_queue(
+        "subjective::design_coherence",
+        "subjective::error_consistency",
+    )
+    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
+
+    auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
+    assert set(plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]["finding_ids"]) == {
+        "subjective::design_coherence",
+        "subjective::error_consistency",
+    }
+
+    # A third dimension becomes stale
+    plan["queue_order"].append("subjective::convention_drift")
+    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
+    assert changes >= 1
+    assert "subjective::convention_drift" in plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]["finding_ids"]
+    assert "Re-review 3 stale" in plan["clusters"]["auto/stale-review"]["description"]
+
+
+def test_single_stale_dim_no_cluster():
+    """Only 1 stale dim → no cluster created (below _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE)."""
+    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
+
+    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")
+    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence")
+
+    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
+    assert "auto/stale-review" not in plan["clusters"]

tool_result Read
```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    12→
    13→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    14→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    15→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    16→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    17→
    18→
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→# Grouping key computation
    21→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22→
    23→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    24→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    25→
    26→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    27→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    28→    """
    29→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    30→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    31→    if kind:
    32→        return kind
    33→
    34→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    35→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    36→    parts = fid.split("::")
    37→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    38→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    39→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    40→        candidate = parts[-1]
    41→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    42→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    43→            return candidate
    44→    return None
    45→
    46→
    47→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    48→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    49→
    50→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    51→    """
    52→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    53→
    54→    if meta is None:
    55→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    56→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    57→
    58→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    59→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    60→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    61→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    64→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    65→
    66→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    67→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    68→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    69→    ):
    70→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    71→        if file:
    72→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    73→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    74→
    75→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    76→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    77→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    78→        if subtype:
    79→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    80→
    81→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    82→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    84→
    85→    # Everything else → by detector
    86→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    87→
    88→
    89→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    90→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    91→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    92→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    93→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
    94→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
    95→    # detector::security → auto/security
    96→    parts = key.split("::")
    97→    if len(parts) == 2:
    98→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    99→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   100→        if prefix_type == "review":
   101→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   102→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   103→    if len(parts) == 3:
   104→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   105→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   106→
   107→
   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→# Description and action generation
   110→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→
   112→def _generate_description(
   113→    cluster_name: str,
   114→    members: list[dict],
   115→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   116→    subtype: str | None,
   117→) -> str:
   118→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   119→    count = len(members)
   120→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   121→
   122→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   123→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   124→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   125→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   126→
   127→    if detector == "structural":
   128→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   129→        if len(files) == 1:
   130→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   131→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   132→
   133→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   134→    if subtype:
   135→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   136→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   137→
   138→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   139→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   140→
   141→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   145→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   146→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   147→        return None
   148→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   149→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   150→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   151→        return fixer_name
   152→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   153→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   154→        if subtype in fixer:
   155→            return fixer
   156→    return None
   157→
   158→
   159→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   160→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   161→
   162→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   163→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   164→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   165→    """
   166→    if " — " in guidance:
   167→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   168→    return guidance
   169→
   170→
   171→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   172→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   173→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   174→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   175→}
   176→
   177→
   178→def _generate_action(
   179→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   180→    subtype: str | None,
   181→) -> str:
   182→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   183→
   184→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   185→    """
   186→    if meta is None:
   187→        return "review and fix each finding"
   188→
   189→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   190→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   191→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   192→        if matched_fixer:
   193→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   194→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   195→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   196→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   197→
   198→    if meta.tool == "move":
   199→        return "desloppify move"
   200→
   201→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   202→    if meta.guidance:
   203→        if subtype:
   204→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   205→        return meta.guidance
   206→
   207→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   208→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   209→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→# Main algorithm
   215→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   216→
   217→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   218→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   219→
   220→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   221→    """
   222→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   223→    changes = 0
   224→
   225→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   226→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   227→
   228→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   229→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   230→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   231→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   232→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   233→
   234→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   235→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   236→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   237→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   238→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   239→            continue
   240→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   241→            continue
   242→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   243→            continue
   244→
   245→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   246→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   247→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   248→        if key is None:
   249→            continue
   250→
   251→        groups[key].append(fid)
   252→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   253→
   254→    # Drop singleton groups
   255→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   256→
   257→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   258→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   259→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   260→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   261→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   262→            if ck:
   263→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   264→
   265→    now = utc_now()
   266→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   267→
   268→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   269→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   270→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   271→
   272→        # Representative finding for metadata
   273→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   274→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   275→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   276→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   277→
   278→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   279→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   280→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   281→
   282→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   283→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   284→
   285→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   286→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   287→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   288→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   289→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   290→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   291→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   292→                if new_ids:
   293→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   294→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   295→                    changes += 1
   296→            else:
   297→                # Replace membership wholesale
   298→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   299→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   300→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   301→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   302→                    cluster["description"] = description
   303→                    cluster["action"] = action
   304→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   305→                    changes += 1
   306→        else:
   307→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   308→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   309→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   310→                # Append a disambiguator
   311→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   312→
   313→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   314→                "name": cluster_name,
   315→                "description": description,
   316→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   317→                "created_at": now,
   318→                "updated_at": now,
   319→                "auto": True,
   320→                "cluster_key": key,
   321→                "action": action,
   322→                "user_modified": False,
   323→            }
   324→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   325→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   326→            changes += 1
   327→
   328→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   329→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   330→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   331→        for fid in member_ids:
   332→            if fid not in overrides:
   333→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   334→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   335→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   336→
   337→    # --- Stale subjective dimension cluster --------------------------------
   338→    subjective_ids = sorted(
   339→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   340→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if len(subjective_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   344→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   345→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in subjective_ids]
   346→        description = f"Re-review {len(subjective_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   347→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   348→
   349→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   350→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   351→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   352→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   353→            new_ids = set(subjective_ids)
   354→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   355→                cluster["finding_ids"] = subjective_ids
   356→                cluster["description"] = description
   357→                cluster["action"] = action
   358→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   359→                changes += 1
   360→        else:
   361→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   362→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   363→                "description": description,
   364→                "finding_ids": subjective_ids,
   365→                "created_at": now,
   366→                "updated_at": now,
   367→                "auto": True,
   368→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   369→                "action": action,
   370→                "user_modified": False,
   371→            }
   372→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   373→            changes += 1
   374→
   375→        # Track cluster membership in overrides (same as main loop)
   376→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   377→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   378→        for fid in subjective_ids:
   379→            if fid not in overrides:
   380→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   381→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   382→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   383→
   384→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   385→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   386→        cluster = clusters[name]
   387→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   388→            continue
   389→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   390→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   391→            continue
   392→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   393→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   394→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   395→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   396→            if alive:
   397→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   398→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   399→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   400→                    changes += 1
   401→                continue
   402→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   403→        # Delete stale cluster
   404→        del clusters[name]
   405→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   406→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   407→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   408→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   409→                override["cluster"] = None
   410→                override["updated_at"] = now
   411→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   412→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   413→        changes += 1
   414→
   415→    plan["updated"] = now
   416→    return changes
   417→
   418→
   419→__all__ = [
   420→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   421→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   422→]
   423→
```

Now I have a comprehensive picture. Here is my summary:

---

## Summary of the "End-of-Batch Stale/Subjective Refresh" Feature

### Overview

This diff implements a system where, when the user works through the prioritized work queue and resolves all the objective (mechanical/detector-based) findings, the system automatically detects that the batch is "done" and injects stale subjective dimensions (review-based quality assessments that have gone out of date) into the queue as the next work items. These stale items are also automatically grouped into clusters for easier batch processing.

### 1. How "End of Batch" / "All Objective Items Done" Is Detected

The key logic lives in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`, in the `sync_stale_dimensions()` function.

**Old behavior:** Stale subjective dimensions were only injected when the queue was completely empty (`if not order and stale_ids`).

**New behavior:** The condition was relaxed. Instead of requiring a totally empty queue, the system now checks whether any *objective* (non-subjective) items remain:

```python
has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
```

This means injection happens when:
- There are zero objective finding IDs left in `queue_order`, AND
- There are stale subjective dimensions that need refreshing

The queue can still contain leftover `subjective::*` IDs from a previous round -- the system no longer requires the queue to be literally empty. It only cares that the "real" (objective/detector-based) work is done.

### 2. How Stale/Subjective Items Are Identified

The `_current_stale_ids()` function in `stale_dimensions.py` determines which subjective dimensions are currently stale:

```python
def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    stale: set[str] = set()
    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
        if not entry.get("stale"):
            continue
        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
        if dim_key:
            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    return stale
```

It calls `scorecard_subjective_entries()` which examines each subjective dimension in the scorecard (things like "design_coherence", "error_consistency", "abstraction_fitness"). A dimension is "stale" when `needs_review_refresh` is true in the state's `subjective_assessments` -- meaning the codebase has changed mechanically since the last time that dimension was reviewed, so the review score is outdated.

Each stale dimension gets a synthetic ID like `subjective::design_coherence`.

### 3. How They Are Queued for Refresh

The flow is a two-stage pipeline that runs during `merge_scan_results` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py`:

**Stage 1: Sync stale dimensions** (`sync_stale_dimensions`):
- **Cleanup:** Removes any `subjective::*` IDs from `queue_order` that are no longer stale (the user refreshed them or mechanical evidence changed).
- **Inject:** If no objective items remain in the queue, appends all currently-stale dimension IDs to `queue_order`.

**Stage 2: Auto-cluster** (`auto_cluster_findings` in the new `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`):
- After stale dimensions are injected into the queue, the auto-clustering pass runs.
- It scans `queue_order` for IDs starting with `subjective::`. If there are 2 or more (`_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2`), it creates a special cluster named `auto/stale-review` with `cluster_key = "subjective::stale"`.
- The cluster's description is like "Re-review 3 stale subjective dimensions" and its action is a CLI command: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions design_coherence,error_consistency,...`
- If the stale set changes (dimensions get refreshed or new ones become stale), the cluster membership is updated. If all stale dimensions are refreshed and the queue empties, the stale cluster is deleted.

### 4. Auto-Clustering of All Findings (Not Just Stale)

The `auto_cluster_findings` function does much more than just stale dimensions. It groups all open findings into clusters by computing a grouping key:

- **Review findings** group by dimension (`review::abstraction_fitness`)
- **Structural/responsibility findings** group by file (`file::structural::big_file.py`)  
- **Findings with subtypes** group by detector+subtype (`typed::dict_keys::phantom_read`)
- **Pure auto-fix detectors** group all by detector (`auto::unused`)
- **Everything else** groups by detector name

Each cluster gets:
- A generated description ("Fix 5 phantom_read issues")
- An action command (e.g., `desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run` for auto-fixable, or guidance text)
- An `action_type` classification: `auto_fix`, `reorganize`, `refactor`, or `manual_fix`

### 5. Cluster Presentation in the Work Queue

In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`, a new `_collapse_clusters` function replaces individual cluster member items with single "cluster meta-items" before they are shown to the user. This happens when:
- `collapse_clusters` is True (default)
- A plan exists with clusters
- The user is NOT already drilling into a specific cluster

The meta-items carry `kind: "cluster"` and contain the members, summary, primary command, and action type.

**Sorting** (in `ranking.py`): Cluster meta-items sort before all individual findings, ordered by action type priority (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix), then by size (bigger clusters first).

**Tier counting**: When counting findings per tier, cluster members are expanded so each member counts at its own tier.

**Rendering** (in `render.py`): A dedicated `_render_cluster_item` function renders cluster task cards showing:
- Action type label and count ("Auto-fixable batch, 12 findings")
- File distribution
- Sample finding IDs
- Primary action command
- Resolution hints (resolve all, drill in, skip cluster)

### 6. Resolve Command: Cluster-Aware Resolution

In `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/apply.py`, a new `_try_expand_cluster` function was added. When a user runs `desloppify resolve fixed "auto/stale-review"`, the system checks if the pattern matches a cluster name and expands it to all member finding IDs, resolving them in batch.

### 7. Suppressed Findings Excluded from Scoring

Three files got the same fix -- suppressed findings are now filtered out early:

- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_scoring/detection.py`: `_iter_scoring_candidates` skips `finding.get("suppressed")`
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/scoring.py`: `_count_findings` skips suppressed
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/filtering.py`: `open_scope_breakdown` skips suppressed

Additionally, the old behavior where suppressing a finding would reset its status to "open" (for "score integrity") was removed from both `remove_ignored_findings` and `upsert_findings`. Suppressed findings are now simply invisible to scoring.

### 8. Other Supporting Changes

- **"Deferred" terminology removed:** The old `deferred` list in the plan is fully replaced by the `skipped` map. The `include_deferred` parameter was renamed to `include_skipped`. Backward-compat shims for `plan_deferred` and `total_deferred` were removed.
- **Schema changes:** The `Cluster` TypedDict gained four new fields: `auto`, `cluster_key`, `action`, and `user_modified`, with defaults applied in `ensure_plan_defaults`.
- **`needs_rescan` flag:** When ignoring a pattern, `config["needs_rescan"] = True` is set, and cleared after the next scan merge.
- **Subjective dimension source:** Scoring now uses `load_dimensions_for_lang()` from the data module instead of `HOLISTIC_DIMENSIONS_BY_LANG`, so the full scorecard dimensions (not just the hardcoded per-language list) generate 0-score placeholders.

### Overall Flow

```
User runs scan
  -> merge_scan_results()
    -> Scoring recomputed (suppressed findings excluded)
    -> reconcile_plan_after_scan() removes resolved findings
    -> sync_stale_dimensions():
        1. Prune subjective IDs no longer stale
        2. If no objective items left -> inject stale dimension IDs
    -> auto_cluster_findings():
        - Group all open findings into auto-clusters
        - Create/update/delete auto/stale-review cluster for stale dims
    -> save_plan()

User runs next
  -> build_work_queue()
    -> _apply_plan_order() reorders by plan queue
    -> _collapse_clusters() replaces member items with cluster meta-items
    -> Filter by tier (cluster members expanded for tier matching)
    -> Render: cluster cards shown with action type, file breakdown, commands
```

The net effect: once a user resolves all the concrete, mechanically-detected findings, the system seamlessly transitions to surfacing the stale subjective review dimensions as the next body of work, grouped into a reviewable cluster with a ready-made CLI command.

    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→

    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    76→    if config_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    83→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    84→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    85→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    86→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    87→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    88→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    89→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    90→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    91→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    92→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    93→
    94→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    # Load the living plan
    97→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    98→    plan = load_plan()
    99→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   100→    if (
   101→        plan.get("queue_order")
   102→        or plan.get("overrides")
   103→        or plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ):
   105→        plan_data = plan
   106→
   107→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   109→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   110→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   111→        if active_cluster:
   112→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   113→
   114→    queue = build_work_queue(
   115→        state,
   116→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   117→            tier=tier,
   118→            count=count,
   119→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   120→            scope=scope,
   121→            status=status,
   122→            include_subjective=True,
   123→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   124→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   125→            explain=explain,
   126→            plan=plan_data,
   127→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   128→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   129→        ),
   130→    )
   131→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   141→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   142→    )
   143→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   144→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   145→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   147→        state,
   148→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   149→    )
   150→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   151→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   152→    ]
   153→    write_query(payload)
   154→
   155→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   156→    if output_file:
   157→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   158→            output_file,
   159→            payload,
   160→            len(items),
   161→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   162→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   163→        ):
   164→            return
   165→        raise SystemExit(1)
   166→
   167→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   171→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   172→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):

   174→
   175→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   176→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(

   178→        state.get("scan_path"),
   179→    )
   180→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   181→    try:

   183→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   184→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   186→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→

     2→

    21→
    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    27→    if not dim_scores:
    28→        return []
    29→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    30→        state,
    31→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    32→    )

    34→
    35→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    36→    findings_scoped: dict,
    37→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    39→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)

    43→    return f"T{tier}"

    46→def _render_tier_navigator(queue: dict) -> None:
    47→    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    48→    print(colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold"))

    50→        colorize(

    52→            f"T3:{counts.get(3, 0)} T4:{counts.get(4, 0)}",

    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(

    60→            "dim",
