```
    64→
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    72→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    73→            print(
    74→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    75→            )
    76→
    77→
    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    81→
    82→
    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    84→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    85→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    86→        return "[review]"
    87→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    88→        return "[auto]"
    89→    return ""
    90→
    91→
    92→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    93→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    94→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    95→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    96→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    97→}
    98→
    99→
   100→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   101→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   102→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   103→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   104→    type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
   105→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
   106→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   107→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   108→
   109→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   110→    members = item.get("members", [])
   111→    if members:
   112→        # File distribution
   113→        from collections import Counter
   114→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   115→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   116→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   117→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   118→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   119→        else:
   120→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   121→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   122→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   123→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   124→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   125→
   126→        # Sample IDs
   127→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   128→        for m in members[:3]:
   129→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   130→        if len(members) > 3:
   131→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   132→
   133→    # Action
   134→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   135→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   136→    if primary_command:
   137→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   138→
   139→    # Resolution hints
   140→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify resolve fixed "{cluster_name}" '
   141→                   '--note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   142→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   143→                   "dim"))
   144→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   145→
   146→
   147→def _render_item(
   148→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   149→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   152→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   153→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   154→        return
   155→
   156→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   157→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   158→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   159→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   160→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   161→
   162→    # Effort/type label
   163→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   164→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   165→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   166→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   167→
   168→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   169→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   170→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   171→        subjective_score = float(
   172→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   173→        )
   174→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   175→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   176→        print(
   177→            colorize(
   178→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   179→                "cyan",
   180→            )
   181→        )
   182→        print(colorize(
   183→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   184→            "dim",
   185→        ))
   186→        if explain:
   187→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   188→                "policy",
   189→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   190→            )
   191→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   195→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   196→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   197→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   198→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   199→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   200→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   201→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   202→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   203→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   204→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   205→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   206→
   207→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   208→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   209→
   210→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   211→    if detail.get("lines"):
   212→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   213→    if detail.get("category"):
   214→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   215→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   216→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   217→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   218→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   219→
   220→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   221→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   222→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   223→        if snippet:
   224→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   225→            print(snippet)
   226→
   227→    if dim_scores:
   228→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   229→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   230→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   231→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   232→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   233→            print(
   234→                colorize(
   235→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   236→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   237→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   238→                    "dim",
   239→                )
   240→            )
   241→
   242→    # Score impact estimate
   243→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   244→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   245→        try:
   246→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   247→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   248→            if impact > 0:
   249→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   250→            else:
   251→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   252→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   253→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   254→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   255→                    if issues > 1:
   256→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   257→                        if bulk > 0:
   258→                            print(colorize(
   259→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   260→                                "cyan",
   261→                            ))
   262→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   263→            pass
   264→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   265→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   266→        try:
   267→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   268→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   269→            if dim_key:
   270→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   271→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   272→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   273→                        continue
   274→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   275→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   276→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   277→                        if drag > 0.01:
   278→                            print(colorize(
   279→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   280→                                "cyan",
   281→                            ))
   282→                        break
   283→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   284→            pass
   285→
   286→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   287→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   288→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   289→        similar_count = sum(
   290→            1
   291→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   292→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   293→        )
   294→        if similar_count > 1:
   295→            print(
   296→                colorize(
   297→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   298→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   299→                    "cyan",
   300→                )
   301→            )
   302→    if explain:
   303→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   304→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   305→        base = (
   306→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   307→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   308→        )
   309→
   310→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   311→        if dim_scores and detector:
   312→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   313→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   314→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   315→                base += (
   316→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   317→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   318→                )
   319→
   320→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   321→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   322→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   323→            if dim_key:
   324→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   325→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   326→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   327→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   328→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   329→                        break
   330→
   331→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   332→        if policy:
   333→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   334→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   335→
   336→
   337→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   338→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   339→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   340→        return
   341→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   342→    if not explain:
   343→        return
   344→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   345→    if available:
   346→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   347→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   348→
   349→
   350→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   351→    if queue.get("items"):
   352→        return False
   353→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   354→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   355→    if tier is not None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   357→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   358→        if available:
   359→            commands = " | ".join(
   360→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   361→            )
   362→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   363→    return True
   364→
   365→
   366→def render_terminal_items(
   367→    items: list[dict],
   368→    dim_scores: dict,
   369→    findings_scoped: dict,
   370→    *,
   371→    group: str,
   372→    explain: bool,
   373→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   374→    plan: dict | None = None,
   375→) -> None:
   376→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   377→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   378→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   379→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   380→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   381→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   382→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   383→
   384→    if group != "item":
   385→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   386→        return
   387→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   388→        if idx > 0:
   389→            print()
   390→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   391→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   392→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   393→        elif len(items) > 1:
   394→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   395→        else:
   396→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   397→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   398→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   399→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   400→
   401→
   402→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   403→    if len(items) != 1:
   404→        return
   405→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   406→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   407→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   408→        return
   409→    item = items[0]
   410→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   411→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   412→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   413→        primary = item.get(
   414→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   415→        )
   416→        print(f"    {primary}")
   417→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   418→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   422→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   423→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   424→        print(f"    {primary}")
   425→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   426→    else:
   427→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   428→
   429→    print(
   430→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   431→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   432→    )
   433→    print(
   434→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   435→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def render_followup_nudges(
   440→    state: dict,
   441→    dim_scores: dict,
   442→    findings_scoped: dict,
   443→    *,
   444→    strict_score: float | None,
   445→    target_strict_score: float,
   446→) -> None:
   447→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   448→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   449→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   450→        state,
   451→        subjective_entries,
   452→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   453→        max_quality_items=3,
   454→        max_integrity_items=5,
   455→    )
   456→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   457→    if strict_score is not None:
   458→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   459→        if gap > 0:
   460→            print(
   461→                colorize(
   462→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   463→                    "cyan",
   464→                )
   465→            )
   466→        else:
   467→            print(
   468→                colorize(
   469→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   470→                    "green",
   471→                )
   472→            )
   473→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   474→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   475→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   476→
   477→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   478→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   479→        findings_scoped
   480→    )
   481→    parts: list[str] = []
   482→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   483→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   484→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   485→    open_review = [
   486→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   487→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   488→    ]
   489→    if low_dims:
   490→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   491→    if stale_count:
   492→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   493→    if unassessed_count:
   494→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   495→    if len(open_review):
   496→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   497→    if coverage_open > 0:
   498→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   499→
   500→    if parts:
   501→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   502→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = [
   506→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   507→    "render_followup_nudges",
   508→    "render_queue_header",
   509→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   510→    "render_terminal_items",
   511→    "scorecard_subjective",
   512→    "show_empty_queue",
   513→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   514→]
   515→
```

```
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_skipped: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    43→
    44→
    45→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    46→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    47→
    48→    items: list[dict]
    49→    total: int
    50→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    51→    requested_tier: int | None
    52→    selected_tier: int | None
    53→    fallback_reason: str | None
    54→    available_tiers: list[int]
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   130→            if skip_entry:
   131→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   132→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   133→                if skip_reason:
   134→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   135→
   136→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   137→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   138→    if effective_cluster:
   139→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   140→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   141→        if cluster_member_ids:
   142→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   143→
   144→    return result
   145→
   146→
   147→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   148→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   149→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   150→        return any(
   151→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   152→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   153→        )
   154→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   155→
   156→
   157→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   158→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   159→    try:
   160→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   161→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   162→        if meta:
   163→            return meta.action_type
   164→    except ImportError:
   165→        pass
   166→    return "manual_fix"
   167→
   168→
   169→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   170→
   171→
   172→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   174→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   175→    if not clusters:
   176→        return items
   177→
   178→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   179→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   180→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   181→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   182→            continue
   183→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   184→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   185→
   186→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   187→        return items
   188→
   189→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   190→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   191→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   192→
   193→    for item in items:
   194→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   195→        if cname:
   196→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   197→        else:
   198→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   199→
   200→    # Build cluster meta-items
   201→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   202→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   203→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   204→        if len(members) < 2:
   205→            result.extend(members)
   206→            continue
   207→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   208→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   209→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   210→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   211→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   212→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   213→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   214→            action_type = "reorganize"
   215→        else:
   216→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   217→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   218→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   219→                action_type = "refactor"
   220→
   221→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   222→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   223→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   224→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   225→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   226→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   227→        else:
   228→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   229→
   230→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   231→        if not primary_command:
   232→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   233→
   234→        meta_item: dict = {
   235→            "id": cname,
   236→            "kind": "cluster",
   237→            "action_type": action_type,
   238→            "summary": summary,
   239→            "members": members,
   240→            "member_count": len(members),
   241→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   242→            "cluster_name": cname,
   243→            "cluster_auto": True,
   244→            "confidence": "high",
   245→            "detector": detector,
   246→            "file": "",
   247→        }
   248→        result.append(meta_item)
   249→
   250→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   251→    return result
   252→
   253→
   254→def build_work_queue(
   255→    state: StateModel,
   256→    *,
   257→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   258→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   259→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   260→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   261→
   262→    status = resolved_options.status
   263→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   264→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   265→    try:
   266→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   267→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   268→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   269→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   270→
   271→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   272→        state,
   273→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   274→        status_filter=status,
   275→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   276→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   277→    )
   278→
   279→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   280→    if (
   281→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   282→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   283→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   284→    ):
   285→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   286→            state,
   287→            state.get("findings", {}),
   288→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   289→        )
   290→        for item in subjective_items:
   291→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   292→                all_items.append(item)
   293→
   294→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   295→
   296→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   297→    if resolved_options.plan:
   298→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   299→            all_items,
   300→            resolved_options.plan,
   301→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   302→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   303→        )
   304→
   305→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   306→    should_collapse = (
   307→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   308→        and resolved_options.plan
   309→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   310→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   311→    )
   312→    if should_collapse:
   313→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   314→
   315→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   316→
   317→    requested_tier = (
   318→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   319→    )
   320→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   321→    fallback_reason = None
   322→    filtered = all_items
   323→
   324→    if requested_tier is not None:
   325→        filtered = [
   326→            item
   327→            for item in all_items
   328→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   329→        ]
   330→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   331→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   332→            if chosen is not None:
   333→                selected_tier = chosen
   334→                filtered = [
   335→                    item
   336→                    for item in all_items
   337→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   338→                ]
   339→                fallback_reason = (
   340→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   341→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   342→                )
   343→        elif not filtered:
   344→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   345→
   346→    total = len(filtered)
   347→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   348→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   349→
   350→    if resolved_options.explain:
   351→        for item in filtered:
   352→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   353→
   354→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   355→    return {
   356→        "items": filtered,
   357→        "total": total,
   358→        "tier_counts": counts,
   359→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   360→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   361→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   362→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   363→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   364→    }
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   369→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   370→    "WorkQueueResult",
   371→    "build_work_queue",
   372→    "group_queue_items",
   373→]
   374→
```

```
1→"""Sync stale subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Handles both directions:
     4→  1. **Cleanup** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale.
     5→  2. **Inject**  — when no objective items remain after cleanup, add stale dimensions.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→
    15→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    20→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    21→
    22→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→
    25→    @property
    26→    def changes(self) -> int:
    27→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    32→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    33→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    34→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    38→    if not dim_scores:
    39→        return set()
    40→
    41→    stale: set[str] = set()
    42→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    43→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    44→            continue
    45→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    46→        if dim_key:
    47→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    48→    return stale
    49→
    50→
    51→def sync_stale_dimensions(
    52→    plan: PlanModel,
    53→    state: StateModel,
    54→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    55→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
    56→
    57→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
    58→       longer stale (the user refreshed them, or mechanical evidence changed).
    59→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
    60→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    61→    """
    62→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    63→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    64→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    65→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    66→
    67→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    68→    to_remove: list[str] = [
    69→        fid for fid in order
    70→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in stale_ids
    71→    ]
    72→    for fid in to_remove:
    73→        order.remove(fid)
    74→        result.pruned.append(fid)
    75→
    76→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
    77→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
    78→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
    79→        existing = set(order)
    80→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
    81→            if sid not in existing:
    82→                order.append(sid)
    83→                result.injected.append(sid)
    84→
    85→    return result
    86→
    87→
    88→__all__ = [
    89→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    90→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    91→]
    92→
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    12→
    13→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    14→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    15→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    16→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    17→
    18→
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→# Grouping key computation
    21→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22→
    23→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    24→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    25→
    26→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    27→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    28→    """
    29→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    30→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    31→    if kind:
    32→        return kind
    33→
    34→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    35→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    36→    parts = fid.split("::")
    37→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    38→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    39→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    40→        candidate = parts[-1]
    41→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    42→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    43→            return candidate
    44→    return None
    45→
    46→
    47→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    48→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    49→
    50→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    51→    """
    52→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    53→
    54→    if meta is None:
    55→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    56→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    57→
    58→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    59→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    60→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    61→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    64→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    65→
    66→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    67→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    68→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    69→    ):
    70→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    71→        if file:
    72→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    73→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    74→
    75→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    76→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    77→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    78→        if subtype:
    79→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    80→
    81→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    82→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    84→
    85→    # Everything else → by detector
    86→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    87→
    88→
    89→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    90→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    91→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    92→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    93→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
    94→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
    95→    # detector::security → auto/security
    96→    parts = key.split("::")
    97→    if len(parts) == 2:
    98→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    99→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   100→        if prefix_type == "review":
   101→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   102→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   103→    if len(parts) == 3:
   104→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   105→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   106→
   107→
   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→# Description and action generation
   110→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→
   112→def _generate_description(
   113→    cluster_name: str,
   114→    members: list[dict],
   115→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   116→    subtype: str | None,
   117→) -> str:
   118→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   119→    count = len(members)
   120→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   121→
   122→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   123→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   124→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   125→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   126→
   127→    if detector == "structural":
   128→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   129→        if len(files) == 1:
   130→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   131→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   132→
   133→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   134→    if subtype:
   135→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   136→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   137→
   138→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   139→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   140→
   141→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   145→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   146→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   147→        return None
   148→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   149→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   150→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   151→        return fixer_name
   152→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   153→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   154→        if subtype in fixer:
   155→            return fixer
   156→    return None
   157→
   158→
   159→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   160→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   161→
   162→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   163→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   164→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   165→    """
   166→    if " — " in guidance:
   167→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   168→    return guidance
   169→
   170→
   171→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   172→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   173→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   174→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   175→}
   176→
   177→
   178→def _generate_action(
   179→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   180→    subtype: str | None,
   181→) -> str:
   182→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   183→
   184→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   185→    """
   186→    if meta is None:
   187→        return "review and fix each finding"
   188→
   189→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   190→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   191→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   192→        if matched_fixer:
   193→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   194→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   195→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   196→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   197→
   198→    if meta.tool == "move":
   199→        return "desloppify move"
   200→
   201→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   202→    if meta.guidance:
   203→        if subtype:
   204→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   205→        return meta.guidance
   206→
   207→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   208→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   209→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→# Main algorithm
   215→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   216→
   217→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   218→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   219→
   220→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   221→    """
   222→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   223→    changes = 0
   224→
   225→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   226→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   227→
   228→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   229→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   230→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   231→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   232→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   233→
   234→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   235→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   236→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   237→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   238→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   239→            continue
   240→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   241→            continue
   242→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   243→            continue
   244→
   245→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   246→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   247→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   248→        if key is None:
   249→            continue
   250→
   251→        groups[key].append(fid)
   252→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   253→
   254→    # Drop singleton groups
   255→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   256→
   257→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   258→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   259→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   260→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   261→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   262→            if ck:
   263→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   264→
   265→    now = utc_now()
   266→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   267→
   268→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   269→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   270→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   271→
   272→        # Representative finding for metadata
   273→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   274→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   275→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   276→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   277→
   278→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   279→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   280→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   281→
   282→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   283→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   284→
   285→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   286→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   287→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   288→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   289→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   290→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   291→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   292→                if new_ids:
   293→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   294→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   295→                    changes += 1
   296→            else:
   297→                # Replace membership wholesale
   298→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   299→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   300→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   301→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   302→                    cluster["description"] = description
   303→                    cluster["action"] = action
   304→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   305→                    changes += 1
   306→        else:
   307→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   308→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   309→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   310→                # Append a disambiguator
   311→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   312→
   313→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   314→                "name": cluster_name,
   315→                "description": description,
   316→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   317→                "created_at": now,
   318→                "updated_at": now,
   319→                "auto": True,
   320→                "cluster_key": key,
   321→                "action": action,
   322→                "user_modified": False,
   323→            }
   324→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   325→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   326→            changes += 1
   327→
   328→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   329→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   330→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   331→        for fid in member_ids:
   332→            if fid not in overrides:
   333→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   334→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   335→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   336→
   337→    # --- Stale subjective dimension cluster --------------------------------
   338→    subjective_ids = sorted(
   339→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   340→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if len(subjective_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   344→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   345→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in subjective_ids]
   346→        description = f"Re-review {len(subjective_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   347→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   348→
   349→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   350→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   351→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   352→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   353→            new_ids = set(subjective_ids)
   354→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   355→                cluster["finding_ids"] = subjective_ids
   356→                cluster["description"] = description
   357→                cluster["action"] = action
   358→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   359→                changes += 1
   360→        else:
   361→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   362→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   363→                "description": description,
   364→                "finding_ids": subjective_ids,
   365→                "created_at": now,
   366→                "updated_at": now,
   367→                "auto": True,
   368→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   369→                "action": action,
   370→                "user_modified": False,
   371→            }
   372→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   373→            changes += 1
   374→
   375→        # Track cluster membership in overrides (same as main loop)
   376→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   377→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   378→        for fid in subjective_ids:
   379→            if fid not in overrides:
   380→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   381→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   382→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   383→
   384→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   385→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   386→        cluster = clusters[name]
   387→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   388→            continue
   389→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   390→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   391→            continue
   392→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   393→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   394→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   395→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   396→            if alive:
   397→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   398→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   399→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   400→                    changes += 1
   401→                continue
   402→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   403→        # Delete stale cluster
   404→        del clusters[name]
   405→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   406→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   407→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   408→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   409→                override["cluster"] = None
   410→                override["updated_at"] = now
   411→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   412→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   413→        changes += 1
   414→
   415→    plan["updated"] = now
   416→    return changes
   417→
   418→
   419→__all__ = [
   420→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   421→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   422→]
   423→
```

```
    21→def _resolve_lang_from_state(state: StateModel) -> str | None:
    22→    """Best-effort language detection from state (scan_history > lang_capabilities)."""
    23→    history = state.get("scan_history")
    24→    if isinstance(history, list):
    25→        for entry in reversed(history):
    26→            if isinstance(entry, dict):
    27→                lang = entry.get("lang")
    28→                if isinstance(lang, str) and lang.strip():
    29→                    return lang.strip().lower()
    30→    capabilities = state.get("lang_capabilities")
    31→    if isinstance(capabilities, dict) and len(capabilities) == 1:
    32→        only_lang = next(iter(capabilities.keys()))
    33→        if isinstance(only_lang, str) and only_lang.strip():
    34→            return only_lang.strip().lower()
    35→    return None
    36→
    37→
    38→def _count_findings(findings: dict) -> tuple[dict[str, int], dict[int, dict[str, int]]]:
    39→    """Tally per-status counters and per-tier breakdowns."""
    40→    counters = dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0)
    41→    tier_stats: dict[int, dict[str, int]] = {}
    42→
    43→    for finding in findings.values():
    44→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    45→            continue
    46→        status = finding["status"]
    47→        tier = finding.get("tier", 3)
    48→        counters[status] = counters.get(status, 0) + 1
    49→        tier_counter = tier_stats.setdefault(tier, dict.fromkeys(_EMPTY_COUNTERS, 0))
    50→        tier_counter[status] = tier_counter.get(status, 0) + 1
    51→
    52→    return counters, tier_stats

    55→def _coerce_subjective_score(value: dict | float | int | str | None) -> float:
    56→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float."""
    57→    raw = value.get("score", 0) if isinstance(value, dict) else value
    58→    try:
    59→        score = float(raw)
    60→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    61→        score = 0.0
    62→    return max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _subjective_target_matches(
    66→    subjective_assessments: dict, *, target: float
    67→) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Return dimension keys whose subjective score matches the target band."""
    69→    matches = [
    70→        dimension
    71→        for dimension, payload in subjective_assessments.items()
    72→        if matches_target_score(_coerce_subjective_score(payload), target)
    73→    ]
    74→    return sorted(matches)

    77→def _subjective_integrity_baseline(target: float | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    78→    """Create baseline subjective-integrity metadata for scan/reporting output."""
    79→    return {
    80→        "status": "disabled" if target is None else "pass",
    81→        "target_score": None if target is None else round(float(target), 2),

    88→def _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
    89→    subjective_assessments: dict,
    90→    *,
    91→    target: float,
    92→) -> tuple[dict, dict[str, object]]:
    93→    """Apply anti-gaming penalties for subjective scores clustered on the target."""
    94→    normalized_target = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(target)))
    95→    matched_dimensions = _subjective_target_matches(
    96→        subjective_assessments,
    97→        target=normalized_target,

    99→    meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(normalized_target)
   100→    meta["matched_count"] = len(matched_dimensions)
   101→    meta["matched_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions
   102→
   103→    if len(matched_dimensions) < _SUBJECTIVE_TARGET_RESET_THRESHOLD:
   104→        meta["status"] = "warn" if matched_dimensions else "pass"
   105→        return subjective_assessments, meta
   106→
   107→    adjusted = deepcopy(subjective_assessments)
   108→    for dimension in matched_dimensions:
   109→        payload = adjusted.get(dimension)
   110→        if isinstance(payload, dict):

   117→            }
   118→
   119→    meta["status"] = "penalized"
   120→    meta["reset_dimensions"] = matched_dimensions

   224→            summary = str(reduced.get("summary", "") or "")
   225→            detector_meta["coverage_status"] = status

   227→            detector_meta["coverage_summary"] = summary
   228→            impacts.append(

   274→def _update_objective_health(
   275→    state: StateModel,
   276→    findings: dict,
   277→    *,
   278→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   279→) -> None:
   280→    """Compute canonical score trio from dimension scoring."""
   281→    pots = state.get("potentials", {})
   282→    if not pots:
   283→        return
   284→
   285→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with desloppify.scoring
   286→    # (scoring -> _scoring/results/core -> _scoring/subjective/core -> review -> state -> _state/merge -> _state/scoring)
   287→    from desloppify.scoring import (
   288→        compute_health_score,
   289→        compute_score_bundle,
   290→        merge_potentials,
   291→    )
   292→
   293→    merged = merge_potentials(pots)
   294→    if not merged:
   295→        return
   296→
   297→    subjective_assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or None
   298→    integrity_target = (
   299→        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(subjective_integrity_target)))
   300→        if isinstance(subjective_integrity_target, int | float)
   301→        else None
   302→    )
   303→    integrity_meta = _subjective_integrity_baseline(integrity_target)
   304→    if subjective_assessments and integrity_target is not None:
   305→        subjective_assessments, integrity_meta = _apply_subjective_integrity_policy(
   306→            subjective_assessments,
   307→            target=integrity_target,
   308→        )
   309→    state["subjective_integrity"] = integrity_meta
   310→
   311→    has_active_checks = any((count or 0) > 0 for count in merged.values())
   312→    if not has_active_checks and not subjective_assessments:
   313→        state["dimension_scores"] = {}
   314→        state["overall_score"] = 100.0
   315→        state["objective_score"] = 100.0
   316→        state["strict_score"] = 100.0
   317→        state["verified_strict_score"] = 100.0
   318→        return
   319→
   320→    # Use the full scorecard dimensions for 0-score placeholders so every
   321→    # dimension the review workflow covers gets a placeholder when unreviewed.
   322→    # Explicit assessments outside this set still count
   323→    # (append_subjective_dimensions handles that).
   324→    allowed_subjective: set[str] | None = None
   325→    lang_name = _resolve_lang_from_state(state)
   326→    if lang_name:
   327→        try:
   328→            from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
   329→                load_dimensions_for_lang,
   330→            )
   331→
   332→            dims, _, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(lang_name)
   333→            if dims:
   334→                allowed_subjective = set(dims)
   335→        except (ImportError, AttributeError):
   336→            pass
   337→
   338→    bundle = compute_score_bundle(
   339→        findings,
   340→        merged,
   341→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   342→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective,
   343→    )
   344→    lenient_scores = bundle.dimension_scores
   345→    strict_scores = bundle.strict_dimension_scores
   346→    verified_strict_scores = bundle.verified_strict_dimension_scores
   347→
   348→    prev_dim_scores = dict(state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   349→
   350→    state["dimension_scores"] = {
   351→        name: dict(
   352→            score=lenient_scores[name]["score"],
   353→            strict_score=strict_scores[name]["score"],
   354→            verified_strict_score=verified_strict_scores[name]["score"],
   355→            checks=lenient_scores[name]["checks"],
   356→            issues=lenient_scores[name]["issues"],
   357→            tier=lenient_scores[name]["tier"],
   358→            detectors=lenient_scores[name].get("detectors", {}),
   359→        )
   360→        for name in lenient_scores
   361→    }
   362→    for data in state["dimension_scores"].values():
   363→        data["strict"] = data["strict_score"]
   364→
   365→    # Carry forward mechanical dimensions from a prior scan that are absent
   366→    # now (e.g. duplication when --skip-slow is used).
   367→    for dim_name, prev_data in prev_dim_scores.items():
   368→        if dim_name in state["dimension_scores"]:
   369→            continue
   370→        if not isinstance(prev_data, dict):
   371→            continue
   372→        if "subjective_assessment" in prev_data.get("detectors", {}):
   373→            continue
   374→        carried = {**prev_data, "carried_forward": True}
   375→        # Backfill for state files written before verified_strict_score existed.
   376→        carried.setdefault(
   377→            "verified_strict_score",
   378→            carried.get("strict_score", carried.get("strict", carried.get("score", 0.0))),
   379→        )
   380→        state["dimension_scores"][dim_name] = carried
   381→
   382→    _apply_scan_coverage_to_dimension_scores(
   383→        state,
   384→        dimension_scores=state["dimension_scores"],
   385→    )
   386→
   387→    state.update(_aggregate_scores(state["dimension_scores"], compute_health_score))
   388→
   389→
   390→def _recompute_stats(
   391→    state: StateModel,
   392→    scan_path: str | None = None,
   393→    *,
   394→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None,
   395→) -> None:
   396→    """Recompute stats and canonical health scores from findings."""
   397→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   398→    findings = path_scoped_findings(state["findings"], scan_path)
   399→    counters, tier_stats = _count_findings(findings)
   400→    state["stats"] = {
   401→        "total": sum(counters.values()),
   402→        **counters,
   403→        "by_tier": {
   404→            str(tier): tier_counts for tier, tier_counts in sorted(tier_stats.items())
   405→        },
   406→    }
   407→    _update_objective_health(
   408→        state,
   409→        findings,
   410→        subjective_integrity_target=subjective_integrity_target,
   411→    )
   412→
   413→
   414→def _empty_suppression_metrics() -> dict[str, int | float]:
   415→    return {
   416→        "last_ignored": 0,
   417→        "last_raw_findings": 0,
   418→        "last_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   419→        "last_ignore_patterns": 0,
   420→        "recent_scans": 0,
   421→        "recent_ignored": 0,
   422→        "recent_raw_findings": 0,
   423→        "recent_suppressed_pct": 0.0,
   424→    }
   425→
   426→
   427→def suppression_metrics(state: StateModel, *, window: int = 5) -> dict[str, int | float]:
   428→    """Summarize ignore suppression from recent scan history."""
   429→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
   430→    if not history:
   431→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   432→
   433→    scans_with_suppression = [
   434→        entry
   435→        for entry in history
   436→        if isinstance(entry, dict)
   437→        and (
   438→            "ignored" in entry
   439→            or "raw_findings" in entry
   440→            or "suppressed_pct" in entry
   441→            or "ignore_patterns" in entry
   442→        )
   443→    ]
   444→    if not scans_with_suppression:
   445→        return _empty_suppression_metrics()
   446→
   447→    recent = scans_with_suppression[-max(1, window) :]
   448→    last = recent[-1]
   449→
   450→    recent_ignored = sum(int(entry.get("ignored", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   451→    recent_raw = sum(int(entry.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0) for entry in recent)
   452→    recent_pct = round(recent_ignored / recent_raw * 100, 1) if recent_raw else 0.0
   453→
   454→    last_ignored = int(last.get("ignored", 0) or 0)
   455→    last_raw = int(last.get("raw_findings", 0) or 0)
   456→    if "suppressed_pct" in last:
   457→        last_pct = round(float(last.get("suppressed_pct") or 0.0), 1)
   458→    else:
   459→        last_pct = round(last_ignored / last_raw * 100, 1) if last_raw else 0.0
   460→
   461→    return {
   462→        "last_ignored": last_ignored,
   463→        "last_raw_findings": last_raw,
   464→        "last_suppressed_pct": last_pct,
   465→        "last_ignore_patterns": int(last.get("ignore_patterns", 0) or 0),
   466→        "recent_scans": len(recent),
   467→        "recent_ignored": recent_ignored,
   468→        "recent_raw_findings": recent_raw,
   469→        "recent_suppressed_pct": recent_pct,
   470→    }
   471→
```

```
   144→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   145→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   146→    config: dict[str, object],
   147→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   148→    lang: LangRun | None,
   149→) -> LangRun | None:
   150→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   151→    if not lang:
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   155→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   156→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   157→        lang,
   158→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   159→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   160→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   161→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   162→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   163→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   164→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   165→        ),
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   169→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   170→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   171→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   172→    }
   173→    return runtime_lang

   175→
   176→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   177→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   178→    *,
   179→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   180→) -> int:
   181→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   182→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   183→
   184→    reset_keys = {
   185→        key.strip()
   186→        for key in assessments
   187→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   188→    }
   189→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   190→
   191→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   192→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   193→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   194→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   195→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   196→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   197→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   198→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   199→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   200→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   201→            payload.pop("components", None)
   202→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   203→            continue
   204→        assessments[key] = {
   205→            "score": 0.0,
   206→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   207→            "assessed_at": now,
   208→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   209→            "placeholder": True,
   210→        }
   211→    return len(reset_keys)
   212→
   213→
   214→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   215→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   216→) -> int:
   217→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   218→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   219→
   220→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   221→    expired = 0
   222→    for payload in assessments.values():
   223→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   224→            continue
   225→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   226→            continue
   227→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   228→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   229→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   230→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   231→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   233→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   234→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   235→        payload.pop("components", None)
   236→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   237→        expired += 1
   238→    return expired

   241→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   242→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   243→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   244→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   245→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   246→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   247→    path = Path(args.path)
   248→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   249→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   250→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   251→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   252→        state
   253→    )
   254→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   255→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   256→            state,
   257→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   261→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   262→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   263→
   264→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   265→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   266→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   267→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   268→
   269→    return ScanRuntime(
   270→        args=args,
   271→        state_path=state_file,
   272→        state=state,
   273→        path=path,
   274→        config=config,
   275→        lang=lang,
   276→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   277→        profile=profile,
   278→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   279→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   280→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   281→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   282→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   283→    )
```
