   110→    members = item.get("members", [])
   111→    if members:
   112→        # File distribution
   113→        from collections import Counter
   114→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   115→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   116→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   117→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   118→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   119→        else:
   120→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   121→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   122→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   123→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   124→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   125→
   126→        # Sample IDs
   127→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   128→        for m in members[:3]:
   129→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   130→        if len(members) > 3:
   131→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   132→
   133→    # Action
   134→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   135→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   136→    if primary_command:
   137→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   138→
   139→    # Resolution hints
   140→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify resolve fixed "{cluster_name}" '
   141→                   '--note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   142→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   143→                   "dim"))
   144→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   145→
   146→
   147→def _render_item(
   148→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   149→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   150→) -> None:
   151→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   152→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   153→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   154→        return
   155→
   156→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   157→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   158→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   159→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   160→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   161→
   162→    # Effort/type label
   163→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   164→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   165→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   166→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   167→
   168→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   169→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   170→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   171→        subjective_score = float(
   172→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   173→        )
   174→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   175→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   176→        print(
   177→            colorize(
   178→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   179→                "cyan",
   180→            )
   181→        )
   182→        print(colorize(
   183→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   184→            "dim",
   185→        ))
   186→        if explain:
   187→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   188→                "policy",
   189→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   190→            )
   191→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   195→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   196→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   197→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   198→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   199→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   200→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   201→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   202→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   203→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   204→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   205→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   206→
   207→    print(f"  File: {item.get('file', '')}")
   208→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   209→
   210→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   211→    if detail.get("lines"):
   212→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   213→    if detail.get("category"):
   214→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   215→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   216→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   217→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   218→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   219→
   220→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   221→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   222→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   223→        if snippet:
   224→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   225→            print(snippet)
   226→
   227→    if dim_scores:
   228→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   229→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   230→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   231→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   232→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   233→            print(
   234→                colorize(
   235→                    f"\n  Dimension: {dimension.name} — {dimension_score['score']:.1f}% "
   236→                    f"(strict: {strict_val:.1f}%) "
   237→                    f"({dimension_score['issues']} of {dimension_score['checks']:,} checks failing)",
   238→                    "dim",
   239→                )
   240→            )
   241→
   242→    # Score impact estimate
   243→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   244→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:
   245→        try:
   246→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_score_impact
   247→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   248→            if impact > 0:
   249→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   250→            else:
   251→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   252→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   253→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   254→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   255→                    if issues > 1:
   256→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   257→                        if bulk > 0:
   258→                            print(colorize(
   259→                                f"  Impact: fixing all {issues} {detector} issues → ~+{bulk:.1f} pts",
   260→                                "cyan",
   261→                            ))
   262→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   263→            pass
   264→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   265→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   266→        try:
   267→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   268→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   269→            if dim_key:
   270→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   271→                for entry in breakdown.get("entries", []):
   272→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   273→                        continue
   274→                    entry_key = str(entry.get("name", "")).lower().replace(" ", "_")
   275→                    if entry_key == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   276→                        drag = float(entry.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0)
   277→                        if drag > 0.01:
   278→                            print(colorize(
   279→                                f"  Dimension drag: {entry['name']} costs -{drag:.2f} pts on overall score",
   280→                                "cyan",
   281→                            ))
   282→                        break
   283→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   284→            pass
   285→
   286→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   287→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   288→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   289→        similar_count = sum(
   290→            1
   291→            for finding in findings_scoped.values()
   292→            if finding.get("detector") == detector_name and finding["status"] == "open"
   293→        )
   294→        if similar_count > 1:
   295→            print(
   296→                colorize(
   297→                    f"\n  Auto-fixable: {similar_count} similar findings. "
   298→                    f"Run `{auto_fix_command}` to fix all at once.",
   299→                    "cyan",
   300→                )
   301→            )
   302→    if explain:
   303→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   304→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   305→        base = (
   306→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   307→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   308→        )
   309→
   310→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   311→        if dim_scores and detector:
   312→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   313→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   314→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   315→                base += (
   316→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   317→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   318→                )
   319→
   320→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   321→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   322→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   323→            if dim_key:
   324→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   325→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   326→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   327→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   328→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   329→                        break
   330→
   331→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   332→        if policy:
   333→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   334→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   335→
   336→
   337→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   338→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   339→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   340→        return
   341→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   342→    if not explain:
   343→        return
   344→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   345→    if available:
   346→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   347→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   348→
   349→
   350→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   351→    if queue.get("items"):
   352→        return False
   353→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   354→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   355→    if tier is not None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   357→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   358→        if available:
   359→            commands = " | ".join(
   360→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   361→            )
   362→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   363→    return True
   364→
   365→
   366→def render_terminal_items(
   367→    items: list[dict],
   368→    dim_scores: dict,
   369→    findings_scoped: dict,
   370→    *,
   371→    group: str,
   372→    explain: bool,
   373→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   374→    plan: dict | None = None,
   375→) -> None:
   376→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   377→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   378→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   379→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   380→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   381→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   382→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   383→
   384→    if group != "item":
   385→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   386→        return
   387→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   388→        if idx > 0:
   389→            print()
   390→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   391→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   392→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   393→        elif len(items) > 1:
   394→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   395→        else:
   396→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   397→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   398→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   399→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   400→
   401→
   402→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   403→    if len(items) != 1:
   404→        return
   405→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   406→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   407→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   408→        return
   409→    item = items[0]
   410→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   411→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   412→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   413→        primary = item.get(
   414→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   415→        )
   416→        print(f"    {primary}")
   417→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   418→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   422→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   423→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   424→        print(f"    {primary}")
   425→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   426→    else:
   427→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   428→
   429→    print(
   430→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   431→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   432→    )
   433→    print(
   434→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   435→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def render_followup_nudges(
   440→    state: dict,
   441→    dim_scores: dict,
   442→    findings_scoped: dict,
   443→    *,
   444→    strict_score: float | None,
   445→    target_strict_score: float,
   446→) -> None:
   447→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   448→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   449→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   450→        state,
   451→        subjective_entries,
   452→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   453→        max_quality_items=3,
   454→        max_integrity_items=5,
   455→    )
   456→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   457→    if strict_score is not None:
   458→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   459→        if gap > 0:
   460→            print(
   461→                colorize(
   462→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   463→                    "cyan",
   464→                )
   465→            )
   466→        else:
   467→            print(
   468→                colorize(
   469→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   470→                    "green",
   471→                )
   472→            )
   473→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   474→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   475→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   476→
   477→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   478→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   479→        findings_scoped
   480→    )
   481→    parts: list[str] = []
   482→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   483→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   484→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   485→    open_review = [
   486→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   487→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   488→    ]
   489→    if low_dims:
   490→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   491→    if stale_count:
   492→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   493→    if unassessed_count:
   494→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   495→    if len(open_review):
   496→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   497→    if coverage_open > 0:
   498→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   499→
   500→    if parts:
   501→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   502→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = [
   506→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   507→    "render_followup_nudges",
   508→    "render_queue_header",
   509→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   510→    "render_terminal_items",
   511→    "scorecard_subjective",
   512→    "show_empty_queue",
   513→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   514→]
   515→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error

    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→

1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    scope_matches,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    15→    build_finding_items,
    16→    choose_fallback_tier,
    17→    group_queue_items,
    18→    item_explain,
    19→    item_sort_key,
    20→    tier_counts,
    21→)
    22→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    23→
    24→
    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    28→
    29→    tier: int | None = None
    30→    count: int | None = 1
    31→    scan_path: str | None = None
    32→    scope: str | None = None
    33→    status: str = "open"
    34→    include_subjective: bool = True
    35→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    36→    chronic: bool = False
    37→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    38→    explain: bool = False
    39→    plan: dict | None = None
    40→    include_skipped: bool = False
    41→    cluster: str | None = None
    42→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    43→
    44→
    45→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    46→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    47→
    48→    items: list[dict]
    49→    total: int
    50→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    51→    requested_tier: int | None
    52→    selected_tier: int | None
    53→    fallback_reason: str | None
    54→    available_tiers: list[int]
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   130→            if skip_entry:
   131→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   132→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   133→                if skip_reason:
   134→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   135→
   136→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   137→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   138→    if effective_cluster:
   139→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   140→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   141→        if cluster_member_ids:
   142→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   143→
   144→    return result
   145→
   146→
   147→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   148→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   149→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   150→        return any(
   151→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   152→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   153→        )
   154→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   155→
   156→
   157→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   158→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   159→    try:
   160→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   161→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   162→        if meta:
   163→            return meta.action_type
   164→    except ImportError:
   165→        pass
   166→    return "manual_fix"
   167→
   168→
   169→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   170→
   171→
   172→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   174→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   175→    if not clusters:
   176→        return items
   177→
   178→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   179→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   180→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   181→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   182→            continue
   183→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   184→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   185→
   186→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   187→        return items
   188→
   189→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   190→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   191→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   192→
   193→    for item in items:
   194→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   195→        if cname:
   196→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   197→        else:
   198→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   199→
   200→    # Build cluster meta-items
   201→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   202→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   203→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   204→        if len(members) < 2:
   205→            result.extend(members)
   206→            continue
   207→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   208→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   209→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   210→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   211→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   212→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   213→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   214→            action_type = "reorganize"
   215→        else:
   216→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   217→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   218→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   219→                action_type = "refactor"
   220→
   221→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   222→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   223→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   224→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   225→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   226→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   227→        else:
   228→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   229→
   230→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   231→        if not primary_command:
   232→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   233→
   234→        meta_item: dict = {
   235→            "id": cname,
   236→            "kind": "cluster",
   237→            "action_type": action_type,
   238→            "summary": summary,
   239→            "members": members,
   240→            "member_count": len(members),
   241→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   242→            "cluster_name": cname,
   243→            "cluster_auto": True,
   244→            "confidence": "high",
   245→            "detector": detector,
   246→            "file": "",
   247→        }
   248→        result.append(meta_item)
   249→
   250→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   251→    return result
   252→
   253→
   254→def build_work_queue(
   255→    state: StateModel,
   256→    *,
   257→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   258→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   259→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   260→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   261→
   262→    status = resolved_options.status
   263→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   264→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   265→    try:
   266→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   267→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   268→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   269→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   270→
   271→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   272→        state,
   273→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   274→        status_filter=status,
   275→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   276→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   277→    )
   278→
   279→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   280→    if (
   281→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   282→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   283→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   284→    ):
   285→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   286→            state,
   287→            state.get("findings", {}),
   288→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   289→        )
   290→        for item in subjective_items:
   291→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   292→                all_items.append(item)
   293→
   294→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   295→
   296→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   297→    if resolved_options.plan:
   298→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   299→            all_items,
   300→            resolved_options.plan,
   301→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   302→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   303→        )
   304→
   305→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   306→    should_collapse = (
   307→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   308→        and resolved_options.plan
   309→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   310→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   311→    )
   312→    if should_collapse:
   313→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   314→
   315→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   316→
   317→    requested_tier = (
   318→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   319→    )
   320→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   321→    fallback_reason = None
   322→    filtered = all_items
   323→
   324→    if requested_tier is not None:
   325→        filtered = [
   326→            item
   327→            for item in all_items
   328→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   329→        ]
   330→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   331→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   332→            if chosen is not None:
   333→                selected_tier = chosen
   334→                filtered = [
   335→                    item
   336→                    for item in all_items
   337→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   338→                ]
   339→                fallback_reason = (
   340→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   341→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   342→                )
   343→        elif not filtered:
   344→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   345→
   346→    total = len(filtered)
   347→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   348→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   349→
   350→    if resolved_options.explain:
   351→        for item in filtered:
   352→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   353→
   354→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   355→    return {
   356→        "items": filtered,
   357→        "total": total,
   358→        "tier_counts": counts,
   359→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   360→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   361→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   362→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   363→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   364→    }
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   369→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   370→    "WorkQueueResult",
   371→    "build_work_queue",
   372→    "group_queue_items",
   373→]
   374→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    88→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    89→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    90→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    91→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    92→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    93→        )
    94→        return (
    95→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
    96→            action_pri,
    97→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
    98→            item.get("id", ""),
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   102→        return (
   103→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   104→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   105→            subjective_score_value(item),
   106→            item.get("id", ""),
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   110→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   111→    return (
   112→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   113→        0,
   114→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   115→        -review_weight,
   116→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   117→        item.get("id", ""),
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   122→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   123→
   124→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   125→        return {
   126→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   127→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   128→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   129→            "policy": (
   130→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   131→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   132→            ),
   133→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   134→        }
   135→
   136→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   137→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   138→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   139→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   140→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   141→    if is_subjective:
   142→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   143→    elif is_review:
   144→        ranking_factors = [
   145→            "tier asc",
   146→            "confidence asc",
   147→            "review_weight desc",
   148→            "count desc",
   149→            "id asc",
   150→        ]
   151→    else:
   152→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   153→    explain = {
   154→        "kind": "finding",
   155→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   156→        "confidence": confidence,
   157→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   158→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   159→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   160→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   161→    }
   162→    if is_review:
   163→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   164→    if is_subjective:
   165→        explain["policy"] = (
   166→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   167→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   168→        )
   169→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   170→    return explain
   171→
   172→
   173→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   174→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   175→    for item in items:
   176→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   177→            # Count each member at its own tier
   178→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   179→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   180→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   181→            continue
   182→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   183→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   184→    return counts
   185→
   186→
   187→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   188→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   189→    if not available:
   190→        return None
   191→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   192→
   193→
   194→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   195→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   196→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   197→    for item in items:
   198→        if group == "file":
   199→            key = item.get("file", "")
   200→        elif group == "detector":
   201→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   202→        elif group == "tier":
   203→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   204→        elif group == "cluster":
   205→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   206→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   207→        else:
   208→            key = "items"
   209→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   210→    return grouped
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "build_finding_items",
   215→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "tier_counts",
   219→    "subjective_score_value",
   220→    "group_queue_items",
   221→]
   222→

1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]

1→"""Helper utilities for work queue item construction."""

     8→from desloppify.core.enums import finding_status_tokens

    17→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."
    23→)
    24→
    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _subjective_dimension_aliases(display_name: str) -> set[str]:
   119→    """Return normalized aliases used to match display labels with finding dimension keys."""
   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})

   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):

   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}
   149→
   150→
   151→def primary_command_for_finding(

   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:

   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"

   172→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}

   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {

   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items

## Complete Implementation Details Report

### 1. **Function Signatures**

#### `stale_dimensions.py`:
- **`_current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]`**
  - Extracts stale dimensions from state and returns them as `subjective::<slug>` IDs
  - No parameters besides state
  
- **`sync_stale_dimensions(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult`**
  - Returns a dataclass with `injected: list[str]` and `pruned: list[str]` fields
  - Also has a `changes` property that sums the two lists' lengths

#### `auto_cluster.py`:
- **`auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int`**
  - Returns count of changes (int) made to clusters
  - No other parameters

#### `scan_workflow.py`:
- **`merge_scan_results(runtime: ScanRuntime, findings: list[dict[str, Any]], potentials: dict[str, object], codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None) -> ScanMergeResult`**
  - Returns `ScanMergeResult` dataclass with fields: `diff`, `prev_overall`, `prev_objective`, `prev_strict`, `prev_verified`, `prev_dim_scores`

---

### 2. **`_current_stale_ids` Logic**

```python
def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    )

    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    if not dim_scores:
        return set()

    stale: set[str] = set()
    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
        if not entry.get("stale"):
            continue
        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
        if dim_key:
            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    return stale
```

**Key details:**
- Gets `dimension_scores` from state; returns empty set if missing
- Calls `scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)` — this is the critical function that filters entries
- **Only includes entries where `entry.get("stale")` is truthy**
- Slugifies the dimension key and prefixes it with `"subjective::"`
- Returns a sorted set of these IDs

---

### 3. **Injection Condition in `sync_stale_dimensions`**

```python
has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
    existing = set(order)
    for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
        if sid not in existing:
            order.append(sid)
            result.injected.append(sid)
```

**Exact condition for injection:**
1. **`has_real_items` must be False** — there are NO items in queue that don't start with `"subjective::"`
2. **`stale_ids` must be non-empty** — there are currently stale dimensions to inject
3. **Only appends IDs not already in the queue** (checks `if sid not in existing`)
4. **Sorted order** — `for sid in sorted(stale_ids)`

---

### 4. **Auto-clustering for Stale-Review Cluster**

Lines 337–383 in `auto_cluster.py` handle the stale-review cluster **separately** from regular findings grouping:

```python
# --- Stale subjective dimension cluster --------------------------------
subjective_ids = sorted(
    fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
    if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
)

if len(subjective_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:  # _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
    cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in subjective_ids]
    description = f"Re-review {len(subjective_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
    action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"

    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
        # Update existing cluster
        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
        old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
        new_ids = set(subjective_ids)
        if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
            cluster["finding_ids"] = subjective_ids
            cluster["description"] = description
            cluster["action"] = action
            cluster["updated_at"] = now
            changes += 1
    else:
        # Create new cluster
        clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
            "name": _STALE_NAME,
            "description": description,
            "finding_ids": subjective_ids,
            "created_at": now,
            "updated_at": now,
            "auto": True,
            "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
            "action": action,
            "user_modified": False,
        }
        existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
        changes += 1

    # Track cluster membership in overrides
    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
    for fid in subjective_ids:
        if fid not in overrides:
            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
```

**Key specifics:**
- **Cluster is created only if `len(subjective_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE` (≥ 2)**
- **Finds all `subjective::*` IDs from `plan["queue_order"]`**
- **Builds CLI command**: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <comma-separated-keys>`
- **Uses `_STALE_KEY` as cluster_key** to track it in `existing_by_key` mapping
- **Sets `user_modified: False` so updates replace membership wholesale** (not merge)
- **Updates `overrides[fid]["cluster"]` for every subjective ID** to tie it to the cluster

---

### 5. **Exact Call Order in `scan_workflow.py` (`merge_scan_results`)**

Lines 426–480 show the exact sequence:

```python
# Reconcile living plan + sync stale subjective dimensions
try:
    from desloppify.engine.plan import (
        load_plan,
        reconcile_plan_after_scan,
        save_plan,
        sync_stale_dimensions,
    )

    plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json"
    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
    dirty = False

    # 1. RECONCILIATION (optional, only if plan has user content)
    if (
        plan.get("queue_order")
        or plan.get("overrides")
        or plan.get("clusters")
        or plan.get("skipped")
    ):
        recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, runtime.state)
        if recon.changes:
            dirty = True
        if recon.resurfaced:
            print(...)

    # 2. STALE DIMENSION SYNC (always runs)
    dim_sync = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state)
    if dim_sync.changes:
        dirty = True
    if dim_sync.pruned:
        print(...)
    if dim_sync.injected:
        print(...)

    # 3. AUTO-CLUSTERING (always runs)
    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
    ac_changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, runtime.state)
    if ac_changes:
        dirty = True

    if dirty:
        save_plan(plan, plan_path)
except Exception:
    pass  # Plan reconciliation is best-effort
```

**Call order is rigid:**
1. **`reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)`** — if plan has user content
2. **`sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)`** — always, handles cleanup + injection
3. **`auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)`** — always, generates/updates clusters
4. **`save_plan(plan, plan_path)`** — if any step marked `dirty = True`

---

### 6. **Key Constants**

| Constant | Value | File | Purpose |
|----------|-------|------|---------|
| `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX` | `"subjective::"` | `stale_dimensions.py` | Prefix for all stale dimension IDs |
| `_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE` | `2` | `auto_cluster.py` | Minimum members needed to create a cluster |
| `_STALE_KEY` | `"subjective::stale"` | `auto_cluster.py` | Internal key for tracking stale-review cluster |
| `_STALE_NAME` | `"auto/stale-review"` | `auto_cluster.py` | Human-readable cluster name |
| `AUTO_PREFIX` | `"auto/"` | `auto_cluster.py` | Prefix for all auto-generated cluster names |
| `_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT` | `20` | `scan_workflow.py` | Default wontfix decay scans setting |

---

### 7. **Critical Data Flows**

**State → Plan → Output:**
1. `state["dimension_scores"]` + `scorecard_subjective_entries()` → identifies stale dimensions
2. `sync_stale_dimensions()` → cleans resolved IDs, injects stale IDs into `plan["queue_order"]`
3. `auto_cluster_findings()` → reads `subjective::*` IDs from `plan["queue_order"]`, creates/updates `auto/stale-review` cluster
4. `plan["overrides"][fid]["cluster"]` → bidirectional mapping of findings to clusters

**Injection timing:**
- Stale dimensions are injected **only after reconciliation** (if it ran)
- Auto-clustering reads the potentially-injected IDs immediately afterward
- Both operations mark `dirty`, triggering a single `save_plan()` call

This is a best-effort workflow: all exceptions are caught and silently ignored, allowing partial successes.

## Complete Report: The `next` Command Flow and Subjective Scorecard Entries

### 1. Complete Flow of `cmd_next` (from start to finish)

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py`

The `cmd_next` function (lines 61-79) orchestrates the entire `next` command:

1. **Initialize runtime & state** (lines 63-67)
   - `command_runtime(args)` loads state and config
   - `require_completed_scan(state)` validates a scan has run

2. **Display warnings** (lines 69-77)
   - Tool staleness warning
   - Skill version warning
   - Config staleness warning

3. **Delegate to `_get_items`** (line 79) — the real work happens here

**The `_get_items` function** (lines 82-198) is where the command logic lives:

1. **Parse arguments** (lines 83-92)
   - `tier`, `count`, `scope`, `status`, `group`, `output_format`, `explain`, `no_tier_fallback`, `cluster`, `include_skipped`

2. **Load the living plan** (lines 96-112)
   - Loads plan from `.desloppify/plan.json` via `load_plan()`
   - Sets `effective_cluster` from plan's `active_cluster` if no explicit cluster specified
   - Only uses plan if it has `queue_order`, `overrides`, or `clusters`

3. **Build work queue** (lines 114-130)
   - Calls `build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(...))`
   - Key options:
     - `include_subjective=True` — includes synthetic subjective dimension items
     - `subjective_threshold=target_strict` — filters subjective items below target
     - `plan=plan_data` — applies plan-based ordering and cluster collapsing
     - `include_skipped=False` — excludes skipped items by default
   - Returns `items = queue.get("items", [])`

4. **Compute narrative** (lines 135-138)
   - Calls `compute_narrative(state, context=NarrativeContext(...))`

5. **Build query payload** (lines 140-153)
   - Serializes items via `build_query_payload(queue, items, ...)`
   - Adds overall/objective/strict scores
   - Adds scorecard dimensions
   - Adds subjective measures (filtered from scorecard dimensions where `subjective=True`)

6. **Handle output modes** (lines 155-168)
   - If `--output <file>`: write JSON to file and return
   - If `--format json|md`: emit non-terminal output and return
   - Otherwise: fall through to terminal rendering

7. **Render terminal output** (lines 170-198)
   - `render_queue_header(queue, explain)` — shows tier navigator
   - `show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score)` — early exit if queue is empty
   - `render_terminal_items(items, ...)` — renders each item (handles clusters, findings, subjective dimensions)
   - `render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)` — if 1 item, show resolution command
   - `render_followup_nudges(state, ...)` — shows subjective summary and north-star target
