### 2. How `build_work_queue` Constructs Items and Handles Clusters

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

The `build_work_queue` function (lines 254-364) constructs the final work queue:

1. **Parse options** (lines 260-269)
   - Normalize `subjective_threshold` to [0, 100] range

2. **Build finding items** (lines 271-277)
   - Calls `build_finding_items(state, ...)` which:
     - Scopes findings to `scan_path`
     - Filters by status (`open`, `all`, etc.)
     - Filters by scope pattern (if provided)
     - Skips suppressed/chronic findings
     - Sets `effective_tier` to 4 for subjective findings, otherwise uses actual tier
     - Computes `primary_command` (for fixers, review, or resolve)
   - Returns list of finding items

3. **Add subjective items** (lines 280-292)
   - If `include_subjective=True` and status in `{"open", "all"}` and not `chronic`:
     - Calls `build_subjective_items(state, findings, threshold=subjective_threshold)`
     - These are synthetic queue items representing underperforming subjective dimensions
     - Only added if `strict_score < threshold`

4. **Sort all items** (line 294)
   - Uses `item_sort_key` from `ranking.py` to order items

5. **Apply plan ordering** (lines 296-303)
   - If plan provided, calls `_apply_plan_order(all_items, plan, ...)`
   - Reorders items per plan's `queue_order`
   - Annotates with plan metadata (`plan_description`, `plan_note`, `plan_cluster`, `plan_skipped`)
   - Optionally filters to a specific cluster

6. **Collapse auto-clusters** (lines 305-313)
   - If `collapse_clusters=True` AND plan has clusters AND not drilling into a cluster:
     - Calls `_collapse_clusters(all_items, plan)` to replace cluster members with meta-items
     - Only collapses clusters marked `auto: true`
     - Skips singleton clusters (shows them as individual findings)

7. **Count tiers and handle tier fallback** (lines 315-344)
   - Computes `tier_counts(all_items)` — count of items at each tier
   - If specific tier requested but has 0 items:
     - Tries to fall back to nearest non-empty tier via `choose_fallback_tier`
     - Sets `fallback_reason` message
   - Otherwise, filters to requested tier

8. **Limit count and add explanations** (lines 346-352)
   - Slices to `count` items
   - If `explain=True`, adds `explain` dict to each item via `item_explain(item)`

9. **Return WorkQueueResult** (lines 354-364)
   - Returns dict with:
     - `items` — the filtered/sorted items
     - `total` — total items before count limit
     - `tier_counts` — counts at each tier
     - `requested_tier`, `selected_tier`, `fallback_reason`
     - `available_tiers` — tiers with non-zero count
     - `grouped` — items grouped for alternate output modes

---

### 3. How Clusters are Ranked/Sorted

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py`

The `item_sort_key` function (lines 86-118) provides the sort key:

**For cluster meta-items** (lines 87-99):
```python
return (
    0,  # Clusters sort BEFORE all individual findings
    action_type_priority,  # 0=auto_fix, 1=reorganize, 2=refactor, 3=manual_fix
    -member_count,  # Bigger clusters first (descending)
    cluster_id,  # Alphabetically as tiebreaker
)
```

**For subjective items** (lines 101-107):
```python
return (
    tier,  # Tier first (always 4)
    1,  # Within T4, subjective items sort AFTER mechanical T4 items
    subjective_score_value,  # Lower score = higher priority
    item_id,  # Alphabetically as tiebreaker
)
```

**For mechanical findings** (lines 109-118):
```python
return (
    tier,  # Ascending tier (T1 first, then T2, T3, T4)
    0,  # Within tier, mechanical items sort BEFORE subjective
    confidence_rank,  # CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9) (high=0, medium=1, low=9)
    -review_weight,  # For review findings, higher weight = higher priority
    -count,  # More occurrences first (descending)
    item_id,  # Alphabetically as tiebreaker
)
```

**Tier counts are computed** via `tier_counts(items)` (lines 173-184):
- Counts each item at its `effective_tier`
- For clusters, counts each member individually (not the cluster as a single item)

### 5. Full Signature and Return Shape of `scorecard_subjective_entries`

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/planning/scorecard_projection.py` (lines 70-122)

**Signature:**
```python
def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    state: dict,
    *,
    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
) -> list[dict]:
```

**Return:** List of subjective entry dicts, each with these fields:

| Field | Type | Description |
|-------|------|-------------|
| `name` | str | Display name (e.g., "Elegance", "Abstraction fit") |
| `score` | float | Score value [0-100] |
| `strict` | float | Strict score (may be lower than `score` after integrity penalties) |
| `checks` | int | Number of checks/reviewable items |
| `issues` | int | Number of open issues |
| `tier` | int | Always 4 for subjective (fixed) |
| `placeholder` | bool | True if no assessment data exists yet (unassessed) |
| `stale` | bool | True if `needs_review_refresh=true` in state's subjective_assessments |
| `dimension_key` | str | Canonical dimension key (e.g., `high_level_elegance`) |
| `cli_keys` | list[str] | List of review `--dimensions` CLI keys for this scorecard entry |

**Important:** Each entry is derived from `scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)`, which are rows where the detector includes `subjective_assessment`.

---

### 6. What Fields Are on Each Subjective Entry

**Detailed field breakdown:**

- **`name`** — Display name from scorecard (e.g., "Elegance (combined)", "Abstraction fit")
  
- **`stale`** (lines 103-107)
  - Computed as: `dim_key and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict) and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")`
  - Set to `True` if the dimension's assessment is marked for refresh
  - Used in `build_subjective_items` to tag summary with "[stale — re-review]" and set command to `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <keys>`

- **`placeholder`** (lines 94-101)
  - Computed as: `assessment_meta.get("placeholder") or (score==0 and issues==0 and checks==0)`
  - Set to `True` if:
    - Explicitly marked as placeholder in detector metadata, OR
    - Score/issues/checks are all zero (no assessment data exists)
  - Used in `build_subjective_items` to set command to `desloppify review --prepare` (initial assessment)

- **`dimension_key`** (line 102)
  - Canonical key from detector's `subjective_assessment` metadata
  - Maps scorecard display names to dimension keys used in CLI (`--dimensions`)

- **Other fields** — straightforward scores and metadata passed through from `scorecard_dimension_rows`

---

### 7. How Subjective Items are Created in the Queue

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py` (lines 198-293)

The `build_subjective_items` function creates synthetic queue items:

1. **Filter by threshold** (line 233)
   - Only creates items for entries where `strict_score < threshold`

2. **Determine commands** (lines 251-268):
   - If `placeholder=True` (unassessed): `desloppify review --prepare`
   - Else if `stale=True`: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <cli_keys>`
   - Else if `open_review > 0`: `desloppify show review --status open`
   - Else: `desloppify review --prepare --dimensions <cli_keys>` (or without dimensions if none)

3. **Build the item** (lines 271-292):
   ```python
   {
       "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
       "kind": "subjective_dimension",
       "detector": "subjective_assessment",
       "tier": 4,
       "effective_tier": 4,
       "confidence": "medium",
       "summary": f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}",
       "detail": {
           "dimension_name": name,
           "dimension": dim_key,
           "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
           "strict_score": strict_val,
           "open_review_findings": open_review,
           "cli_keys": cli_keys,
       },
       "status": "open",
       "primary_command": primary_command,
   }
   ```

These synthetic items are added to the work queue if `include_subjective=True` and `strict_score < subjective_threshold`.

## Summary Table: Key Distinctions

| Aspect | Cluster | Subjective Dimension | Regular Finding |
|--------|---------|----------------------|-----------------|
| `kind` | `"cluster"` | `"subjective_dimension"` | `"finding"` |
| `tier` | Varies by members | Always `4` | T1–T4 |
| `effective_tier` | Varies by members | Always `4` | Actual tier |
| `detector` | Original detector | `"subjective_assessment"` | Actual detector |
| Sort priority | 0 (clusters first) | Within T4, after mechanical (1) | Within tier, before subjective (0) |
| Rendering | Special cluster card | Subjective dimension item | Standard item |
| Built from | Plan's auto-clusters | `scorecard_subjective_entries` | State's findings |

I need to design an implementation plan for adding "initial subjective review" support to the desloppify tool. Here's the context:

## Current system (stale reviews at end of batch)
- `stale_dimensions.py` has `_current_stale_ids(state)` which finds dimensions where `entry.get("stale")` is True via `scorecard_subjective_entries()`
- `sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)` appends stale dimension IDs (prefixed `subjective::`) to the BACK of `plan["queue_order"]` ONLY when no objective items remain
- `auto_cluster.py` groups all `subjective::*` IDs from queue_order into an `auto/stale-review` cluster
- `scan_workflow.py` calls: reconcile → sync_stale → auto_cluster → save_plan

## What we want to add
After a scan, if there are subjective dimensions that have NEVER been scored (placeholder=True), they should be injected at the FRONT of the queue so they appear as the user's first priority. This is distinct from stale (which stays at the back, end-of-batch only).

Key requirements:
1. Unscored dimensions go to FRONT of queue (highest priority after scan)
2. Stale dimensions stay at BACK (only when no objective items left) — unchanged
3. A dimension is "unscored" when `scorecard_subjective_entries` returns `placeholder=True` for it
4. A dimension CANNOT be both unscored and stale (disjoint sets)
5. Auto-cluster should create `auto/initial-review` for unscored dims, separate from `auto/stale-review`
6. The injection is unconditional (not gated on "no objective items remaining")

## Files involved
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py` — existing stale sync
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py` — auto clustering, constants: _STALE_KEY="subjective::stale", _STALE_NAME="auto/stale-review", _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE=2
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py` — orchestration
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/plan.py` — facade that exports from _plan subpackage
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` — sort keys for clusters (action_type_priority: auto_fix=0, reorganize=1, refactor=2, manual_fix=3)
- `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` — `_render_cluster_item` maps action_type to labels

## Design questions I want you to address:
1. Should `sync_unscored_dimensions` be a separate function or should we extend `sync_stale_dimensions`?
2. How should `auto_cluster_findings` distinguish unscored vs stale IDs (they both use `subjective::` prefix)?
3. Should we add a new `action_type` for the initial-review cluster, or reuse existing ones?
4. What's the right call order in scan_workflow.py?
5. What about _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE for initial review — should 1 unscored dimension create a cluster?
6. How does the queue ordering work end-to-end: unscored prepended → plan ordering → cluster collapse → rendering?
7. What edge cases matter? (partial reviews, re-scans, language with no subjective dims, etc.)

Please provide a concrete, detailed implementation plan with specific changes per file.

1→"""Sync stale subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Handles both directions:
     4→  1. **Cleanup** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs whose dimension is no longer stale.
     5→  2. **Inject**  — when no objective items remain after cleanup, add stale dimensions.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    11→
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    14→
    15→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    16→
    17→
    18→@dataclass
    19→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    20→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    21→
    22→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    24→
    25→    @property
    26→    def changes(self) -> int:
    27→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    28→
    29→
    30→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    31→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    32→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    33→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    34→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    35→    )
    36→
    37→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    38→    if not dim_scores:
    39→        return set()
    40→
    41→    stale: set[str] = set()
    42→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    43→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    44→            continue
    45→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    46→        if dim_key:
    47→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    48→    return stale
    49→
    50→
    51→def sync_stale_dimensions(
    52→    plan: PlanModel,
    53→    state: StateModel,
    54→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    55→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
    56→
    57→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
    58→       longer stale (the user refreshed them, or mechanical evidence changed).
    59→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
    60→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    61→    """
    62→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    63→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    64→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    65→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    66→
    67→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    68→    to_remove: list[str] = [
    69→        fid for fid in order
    70→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid not in stale_ids
    71→    ]
    72→    for fid in to_remove:
    73→        order.remove(fid)
    74→        result.pruned.append(fid)
    75→
    76→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
    77→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
    78→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
    79→        existing = set(order)
    80→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
    81→            if sid not in existing:
    82→                order.append(sid)
    83→                result.injected.append(sid)
    84→
    85→    return result
    86→
    87→
    88→__all__ = [
    89→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    90→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    91→]
    92→
```

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    11→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    12→
    13→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    14→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    15→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    16→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    17→
    18→
    19→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    20→# Grouping key computation
    21→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    22→
    23→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    24→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    25→
    26→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    27→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    28→    """
    29→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    30→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    31→    if kind:
    32→        return kind
    33→
    34→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    35→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    36→    parts = fid.split("::")
    37→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    38→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    39→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    40→        candidate = parts[-1]
    41→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    42→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    43→            return candidate
    44→    return None
    45→
    46→
    47→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    48→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    49→
    50→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    51→    """
    52→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    53→
    54→    if meta is None:
    55→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    56→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    57→
    58→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    59→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    60→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    61→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    64→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    65→
    66→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    67→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    68→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    69→    ):
    70→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    71→        if file:
    72→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    73→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    74→
    75→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    76→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    77→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    78→        if subtype:
    79→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    80→
    81→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    82→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    84→
    85→    # Everything else → by detector
    86→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    87→
    88→
    89→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    90→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    91→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    92→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    93→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
    94→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
    95→    # detector::security → auto/security
    96→    parts = key.split("::")
    97→    if len(parts) == 2:
    98→        prefix_type = parts[0]
    99→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   100→        if prefix_type == "review":
   101→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   102→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   103→    if len(parts) == 3:
   104→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   105→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   106→
   107→
   108→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   109→# Description and action generation
   110→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   111→
   112→def _generate_description(
   113→    cluster_name: str,
   114→    members: list[dict],
   115→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   116→    subtype: str | None,
   117→) -> str:
   118→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   119→    count = len(members)
   120→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   121→
   122→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   123→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   124→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   125→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   126→
   127→    if detector == "structural":
   128→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   129→        if len(files) == 1:
   130→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   131→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   132→
   133→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   134→    if subtype:
   135→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   136→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   137→
   138→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   139→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   140→
   141→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   142→
   143→
   144→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   145→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   146→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   147→        return None
   148→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   149→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   150→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   151→        return fixer_name
   152→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   153→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   154→        if subtype in fixer:
   155→            return fixer
   156→    return None
   157→
   158→
   159→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   160→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   161→
   162→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   163→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   164→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   165→    """
   166→    if " — " in guidance:
   167→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   168→    return guidance
   169→
   170→
   171→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   172→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   173→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   174→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   175→}
   176→
   177→
   178→def _generate_action(
   179→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   180→    subtype: str | None,
   181→) -> str:
   182→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   183→
   184→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   185→    """
   186→    if meta is None:
   187→        return "review and fix each finding"
   188→
   189→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   190→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   191→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   192→        if matched_fixer:
   193→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   194→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   195→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   196→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   197→
   198→    if meta.tool == "move":
   199→        return "desloppify move"
   200→
   201→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   202→    if meta.guidance:
   203→        if subtype:
   204→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   205→        return meta.guidance
   206→
   207→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   208→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   209→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   210→    )
   211→
   212→
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→# Main algorithm
   215→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   216→
   217→def auto_cluster_findings(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> int:
   218→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   219→
   220→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   221→    """
   222→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   223→    changes = 0
   224→
   225→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   226→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   227→
   228→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   229→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   230→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   231→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   232→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   233→
   234→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   235→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   236→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   237→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   238→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   239→            continue
   240→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   241→            continue
   242→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   243→            continue
   244→
   245→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   246→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   247→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   248→        if key is None:
   249→            continue
   250→
   251→        groups[key].append(fid)
   252→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   253→
   254→    # Drop singleton groups
   255→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   256→
   257→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   258→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   259→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   260→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   261→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   262→            if ck:
   263→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   264→
   265→    now = utc_now()
   266→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   267→
   268→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   269→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   270→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   271→
   272→        # Representative finding for metadata
   273→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   274→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   275→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   276→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   277→
   278→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   279→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   280→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   281→
   282→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   283→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   284→
   285→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   286→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   287→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   288→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   289→                # Merge new findings in, don't remove user's edits
   290→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   291→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   292→                if new_ids:
   293→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   294→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   295→                    changes += 1
   296→            else:
   297→                # Replace membership wholesale
   298→                old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   299→                new_ids = set(member_ids)
   300→                if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   301→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   302→                    cluster["description"] = description
   303→                    cluster["action"] = action
   304→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   305→                    changes += 1
   306→        else:
   307→            # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   308→            # If cluster_name already exists with a different key, skip
   309→            if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   310→                # Append a disambiguator
   311→                cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   312→
   313→            new_cluster: Cluster = {
   314→                "name": cluster_name,
   315→                "description": description,
   316→                "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   317→                "created_at": now,
   318→                "updated_at": now,
   319→                "auto": True,
   320→                "cluster_key": key,
   321→                "action": action,
   322→                "user_modified": False,
   323→            }
   324→            clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   325→            existing_by_key[key] = cluster_name
   326→            changes += 1
   327→
   328→        # Update overrides to track cluster membership
   329→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   330→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(key, cluster_name)
   331→        for fid in member_ids:
   332→            if fid not in overrides:
   333→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   334→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   335→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   336→
   337→    # --- Stale subjective dimension cluster --------------------------------
   338→    subjective_ids = sorted(
   339→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   340→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   341→    )
   342→
   343→    if len(subjective_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   344→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   345→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in subjective_ids]
   346→        description = f"Re-review {len(subjective_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   347→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   348→
   349→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY)
   350→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   351→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   352→            old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   353→            new_ids = set(subjective_ids)
   354→            if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   355→                cluster["finding_ids"] = subjective_ids
   356→                cluster["description"] = description
   357→                cluster["action"] = action
   358→                cluster["updated_at"] = now
   359→                changes += 1
   360→        else:
   361→            clusters[_STALE_NAME] = {
   362→                "name": _STALE_NAME,
   363→                "description": description,
   364→                "finding_ids": subjective_ids,
   365→                "created_at": now,
   366→                "updated_at": now,
   367→                "auto": True,
   368→                "cluster_key": _STALE_KEY,
   369→                "action": action,
   370→                "user_modified": False,
   371→            }
   372→            existing_by_key[_STALE_KEY] = _STALE_NAME
   373→            changes += 1
   374→
   375→        # Track cluster membership in overrides (same as main loop)
   376→        overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   377→        current_name = existing_by_key.get(_STALE_KEY, _STALE_NAME)
   378→        for fid in subjective_ids:
   379→            if fid not in overrides:
   380→                overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   381→            overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   382→            overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   383→
   384→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   385→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   386→        cluster = clusters[name]
   387→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   388→            continue
   389→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   390→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   391→            continue
   392→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   393→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   394→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   395→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   396→            if alive:
   397→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   398→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   399→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   400→                    changes += 1
   401→                continue
   402→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   403→        # Delete stale cluster
   404→        del clusters[name]
   405→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   406→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   407→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   408→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   409→                override["cluster"] = None
   410→                override["updated_at"] = now
   411→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   412→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   413→        changes += 1
   414→
   415→    plan["updated"] = now
   416→    return changes
   417→
   418→
   419→__all__ = [
   420→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   421→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   422→]
   423→
```

   144→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   145→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   146→    config: dict[str, object],
   147→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   148→    lang: LangRun | None,
   149→) -> LangRun | None:
   150→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   151→    if not lang:
   152→        return None
   153→
   154→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   155→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   156→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   157→        lang,
   158→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   159→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   160→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   161→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   162→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   163→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   164→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   165→        ),
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   169→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   170→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   171→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   172→    }
   173→    return runtime_lang
   174→
   175→
   176→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   177→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   178→    *,
   179→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   180→) -> int:
   181→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   182→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   183→
   184→    reset_keys = {
   185→        key.strip()
   186→        for key in assessments
   187→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   188→    }
   189→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   190→
   191→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   192→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   193→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   194→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   195→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   196→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   197→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   198→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   199→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   200→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   201→            payload.pop("components", None)
   202→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   203→            continue
   204→        assessments[key] = {
   205→            "score": 0.0,
   206→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   207→            "assessed_at": now,
   208→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   209→            "placeholder": True,
   210→        }
   211→    return len(reset_keys)
   212→
   213→
   214→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   215→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   216→) -> int:
   217→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   218→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   219→
   220→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   221→    expired = 0
   222→    for payload in assessments.values():
   223→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   224→            continue
   225→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   226→            continue
   227→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   228→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   229→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   230→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   231→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   232→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   233→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   234→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   235→        payload.pop("components", None)
   236→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   237→        expired += 1
   238→    return expired

   241→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   242→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   243→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   244→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   245→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   246→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   247→    path = Path(args.path)
   248→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   249→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   250→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   251→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   252→        state
   253→    )
   254→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   255→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   256→            state,
   257→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   261→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   262→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   263→
   264→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   265→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   266→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   267→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   268→
   269→    return ScanRuntime(
   270→        args=args,
   271→        state_path=state_file,
   272→        state=state,
   273→        path=path,
   274→        config=config,
   275→        lang=lang,
   276→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   277→        profile=profile,
   278→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   279→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   280→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   281→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   282→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   283→    )

   285→
   286→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   287→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   288→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   289→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   290→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   291→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   292→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   293→        return findings
   294→
   295→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   296→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   297→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   298→    )
   299→    if not stale:
   300→        return findings
   301→
   302→    augmented = list(findings)
   303→    augmented.extend(stale)
   304→    for stale_finding in stale:
   305→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   306→    return augmented
   307→
   308→
   309→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   310→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   311→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   312→    *,
   313→    decay_scans: int,
   314→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   315→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   316→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   317→        findings,
   318→        state=runtime.state,
   319→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   320→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   321→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   322→    )
   323→
   324→
   325→def run_scan_generation(
   326→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   327→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   328→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   329→    from desloppify.languages._framework.treesitter import (
   330→        disable_parse_cache,
   331→        enable_parse_cache,
   332→    )
   333→
   334→    enable_file_cache()
   335→    enable_parse_cache()
   336→    try:
   337→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   338→            runtime.path,
   339→            lang=runtime.lang,
   340→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   341→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   342→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   343→                profile=runtime.profile,
   344→            ),
   345→        )
   346→    finally:
   347→        disable_parse_cache()
   348→        disable_file_cache()
   349→
   350→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   351→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   352→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   353→    )
   354→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   355→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   356→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   357→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   358→    )
   359→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   360→        findings,
   361→        runtime,
   362→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   363→    )
   364→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   365→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   366→
   367→
   368→def merge_scan_results(
   369→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   370→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   371→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   372→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   373→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   374→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   375→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   376→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   377→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   378→
   379→    if not path_changed:
   380→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   381→    else:
   382→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   383→    prev_dim_scores = (
   384→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   385→    )
   386→
   387→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   388→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   389→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   390→
   391→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   392→
   393→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   394→        runtime.state,
   395→        findings,
   396→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   397→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   398→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   399→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   400→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   401→            potentials=potentials,
   402→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   403→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   404→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   405→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   406→        ),
   407→    )
   408→
   409→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   410→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   411→    )
   412→    state_mod.save_state(
   413→        runtime.state,
   414→        runtime.state_path,
   415→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   416→    )
   417→
   418→    # Clear needs_rescan flag after successful scan merge (best-effort)
   419→    if runtime.config.get("needs_rescan"):
   420→        try:
   421→            runtime.config["needs_rescan"] = False
   422→            _save_config(runtime.config)
   423→        except OSError:
   424→            pass
   425→
   426→    # Reconcile living plan + sync stale subjective dimensions
   427→    try:
   428→        from desloppify.engine.plan import (
   429→            load_plan,
   430→            reconcile_plan_after_scan,
   431→            save_plan,
   432→            sync_stale_dimensions,
   433→        )
   434→
   435→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   436→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   437→        dirty = False
   438→
   439→        # Standard reconciliation (only if plan has user content)
   440→        if (
   441→            plan.get("queue_order")
   442→            or plan.get("overrides")
   443→            or plan.get("clusters")
   444→            or plan.get("skipped")
   445→        ):
   446→            recon = reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, runtime.state)
   447→            if recon.changes:
   448→                dirty = True
   449→            if recon.resurfaced:
   450→                print(colorize(
   451→                    f"  Plan: {len(recon.resurfaced)} skipped item(s) re-surfaced after review period.",
   452→                    "cyan",
   453→                ))
   454→
   455→        # Stale dimension sync (always — handles both cleanup and injection)
   456→        dim_sync = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state)
   457→        if dim_sync.changes:
   458→            dirty = True
   459→        if dim_sync.pruned:
   460→            print(colorize(
   461→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.pruned)} refreshed subjective dimension(s) removed from queue.",
   462→                "cyan",
   463→            ))
   464→        if dim_sync.injected:
   465→            print(colorize(
   466→                f"  Plan: {len(dim_sync.injected)} stale subjective dimension(s) queued for refresh.",
   467→                "cyan",
   468→            ))
   469→
   470→        # Auto-cluster findings into task groups
   471→        from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
   472→
   473→        ac_changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, runtime.state)
   474→        if ac_changes:
   475→            dirty = True
   476→
   477→        if dirty:
   478→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   479→    except Exception:
   480→        pass  # Plan reconciliation is best-effort
   481→
   482→    return ScanMergeResult(
   483→        diff=diff,
   484→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   485→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   486→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   487→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   488→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   489→    )
   490→
   491→
   492→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   493→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   494→    config: dict[str, object],
   495→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   496→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   497→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   498→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   499→    )
   500→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   501→    open_findings = [
   502→        finding
   503→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   504→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   505→        ).values()
   506→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   507→    ]
   508→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   509→        open_findings,
   510→        budget=noise_budget,
   511→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   512→    )
   513→
   514→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   515→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   516→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   517→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   518→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   519→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   520→    )
   521→
   522→
   523→def persist_reminder_history(
   524→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   525→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   526→) -> None:
   527→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   528→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   529→        return
   530→
   531→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   532→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   533→    state_mod.save_state(
   534→        runtime.state,
   535→        runtime.state_path,
   536→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   537→    )
   538→
   539→
   540→__all__ = [
   541→    "ScanStateContractError",
   542→    "ScanMergeResult",
   543→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   544→    "ScanRuntime",
   545→    "merge_scan_results",
   546→    "persist_reminder_history",
   547→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   548→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   549→    "run_scan_generation",
   550→]

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    88→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
    89→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
    90→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
    91→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
    92→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
    93→        )
    94→        return (
    95→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
    96→            action_pri,
    97→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
    98→            item.get("id", ""),
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   102→        return (
   103→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   104→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   105→            subjective_score_value(item),
   106→            item.get("id", ""),
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   110→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   111→    return (
   112→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   113→        0,
   114→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   115→        -review_weight,
   116→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   117→        item.get("id", ""),
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   122→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   123→
   124→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   125→        return {
   126→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   127→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   128→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   129→            "policy": (
   130→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   131→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   132→            ),
   133→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   134→        }
   135→
   136→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   137→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   138→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   139→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   140→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   141→    if is_subjective:
   142→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   143→    elif is_review:
   144→        ranking_factors = [
   145→            "tier asc",
   146→            "confidence asc",
   147→            "review_weight desc",
   148→            "count desc",
   149→            "id asc",
   150→        ]
   151→    else:
   152→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   153→    explain = {
   154→        "kind": "finding",
   155→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   156→        "confidence": confidence,
   157→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   158→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   159→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   160→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   161→    }
   162→    if is_review:
   163→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   164→    if is_subjective:
   165→        explain["policy"] = (
   166→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   167→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   168→        )
   169→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   170→    return explain
   171→
   172→
   173→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   174→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   175→    for item in items:
   176→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   177→            # Count each member at its own tier
   178→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   179→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   180→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   181→            continue
   182→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   183→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   184→    return counts
   185→
   186→
   187→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   188→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   189→    if not available:
   190→        return None
   191→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   192→
   193→
   194→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   195→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   196→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   197→    for item in items:
   198→        if group == "file":
   199→            key = item.get("file", "")
   200→        elif group == "detector":
   201→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   202→        elif group == "tier":
   203→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   204→        elif group == "cluster":
   205→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   206→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   207→        else:
   208→            key = "items"
   209→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   210→    return grouped
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "build_finding_items",
   215→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   216→    "item_explain",
   217→    "item_sort_key",
   218→    "tier_counts",
   219→    "subjective_score_value",
   220→    "group_queue_items",
   221→]
   222→

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""

    31→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    32→    )
    33→
    34→
    35→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    36→    findings_scoped: dict,
    37→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:

    39→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    40→
    41→
    42→def _tier_label(tier: int) -> str:
    43→    return f"T{tier}"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _render_tier_navigator(queue: dict) -> None:
    47→    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    48→    print(colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold"))
    49→    print(
    50→        colorize(
    51→            f"    Open: T1:{counts.get(1, 0)} T2:{counts.get(2, 0)} "
    52→            f"T3:{counts.get(3, 0)} T4:{counts.get(4, 0)}",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(
    58→            "    Switch: `desloppify next --tier 1` | `desloppify next --tier 2` | "
    59→            "`desloppify next --tier 3` | `desloppify next --tier 4`",
    60→            "dim",

    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    72→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    73→            print(
    74→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"

    77→
    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    81→
    82→
    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    84→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    85→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    86→        return "[review]"
    87→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    88→        return "[auto]"
    89→    return ""

    91→

   100→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   101→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   102→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   103→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   104→    type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")

   106→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   107→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   108→
   109→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   110→    members = item.get("members", [])
   111→    if members:
   112→        # File distribution
   113→        from collections import Counter
   114→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   115→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   116→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   117→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():

   220→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   221→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   222→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   223→        if snippet:

   225→            print(snippet)

   227→    if dim_scores:
   228→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   229→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   230→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:

   232→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   233→            print(

   247→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)

   250→            else:

   264→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:

   337→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   338→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   339→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   340→        return
   341→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   342→    if not explain:
   343→        return
   344→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   345→    if available:
   346→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   347→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   348→
   349→
   350→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   351→    if queue.get("items"):
   352→        return False
   353→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   354→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   355→    if tier is not None:
   356→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   357→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   358→        if available:
   359→            commands = " | ".join(
   360→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   361→            )
   362→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   363→    return True
   364→
   365→
   366→def render_terminal_items(
   367→    items: list[dict],
   368→    dim_scores: dict,
   369→    findings_scoped: dict,
   370→    *,
   371→    group: str,
   372→    explain: bool,
   373→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   374→    plan: dict | None = None,
   375→) -> None:
   376→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   377→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   378→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   379→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   380→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   381→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   382→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({len(items)} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   383→
   384→    if group != "item":
   385→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   386→        return
   387→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   388→        if idx > 0:
   389→            print()
   390→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   391→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   392→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   393→        elif len(items) > 1:
   394→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   395→        else:
   396→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   397→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   398→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   399→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   400→
   401→
   402→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   403→    if len(items) != 1:
   404→        return
   405→    if items[0].get("kind") == "cluster":
   406→        return  # Cluster card already includes resolution hints
   407→    if items[0].get("kind") != "finding":
   408→        return
   409→    item = items[0]
   410→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   411→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   412→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   413→        primary = item.get(
   414→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   415→        )
   416→        print(f"    {primary}")
   417→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   418→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   419→        return
   420→
   421→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   422→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   423→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   424→        print(f"    {primary}")
   425→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   426→    else:
   427→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   428→
   429→    print(
   430→        f'    desloppify resolve fixed "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   431→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   432→    )
   433→    print(
   434→        f'    desloppify resolve wontfix "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   435→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   436→    )
   437→
   438→
   439→def render_followup_nudges(
   440→    state: dict,
   441→    dim_scores: dict,
   442→    findings_scoped: dict,
   443→    *,
   444→    strict_score: float | None,
   445→    target_strict_score: float,
   446→) -> None:
   447→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   448→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   449→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   450→        state,
   451→        subjective_entries,
   452→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   453→        max_quality_items=3,
   454→        max_integrity_items=5,
   455→    )
   456→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   457→    if strict_score is not None:
   458→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   459→        if gap > 0:
   460→            print(
   461→                colorize(
   462→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   463→                    "cyan",
   464→                )
   465→            )
   466→        else:
   467→            print(
   468→                colorize(
   469→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   470→                    "green",
   471→                )
   472→            )
   473→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   474→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   475→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   476→
   477→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   478→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   479→        findings_scoped
   480→    )
   481→    parts: list[str] = []
   482→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   483→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   484→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   485→    open_review = [
   486→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   487→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   488→    ]
   489→    if low_dims:
   490→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   491→    if stale_count:
   492→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   493→    if unassessed_count:
   494→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   495→    if len(open_review):
   496→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   497→    if coverage_open > 0:
   498→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   499→
   500→    if parts:
   501→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   502→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   503→
   504→
   505→__all__ = [
   506→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   507→    "render_followup_nudges",
   508→    "render_queue_header",
   509→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   510→    "render_terminal_items",
   511→    "scorecard_subjective",
   512→    "show_empty_queue",
   513→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   514→]
   515→

    25→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    26→class QueueBuildOptions:
    27→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""

    30→    count: int | None = 1

    44→
    45→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    46→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    47→
    48→    items: list[dict]
    49→    total: int
    50→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
