```
    56→
    57→
    58→def _apply_plan_order(
    59→    items: list[dict],
    60→    plan: dict,
    61→    *,
    62→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    63→    cluster: str | None = None,
    64→) -> list[dict]:
    65→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    66→
    67→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    68→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    69→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    70→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    71→    """
    72→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    73→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    74→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    75→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    76→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    77→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→
    79→    # Build lookup
    80→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    81→    for item in items:
    82→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    83→
    84→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
    85→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
    86→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
    87→        if override.get("description"):
    88→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
    89→        if override.get("note"):
    90→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
    91→        if override.get("cluster"):
    92→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
    93→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
    94→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
    95→                "name": cluster_name,
    96→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
    97→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
    98→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
    99→            }
   100→
   101→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   102→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   103→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   104→    for fid in queue_order:
   105→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   106→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   107→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   108→
   109→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   110→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   111→    for item in items:
   112→        item_id = item["id"]
   113→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   114→            continue
   115→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   116→            skipped_items.append(item)
   117→        else:
   118→            remaining.append(item)
   119→
   120→    # Assign queue positions
   121→    result = ordered + remaining
   122→    if include_skipped:
   123→        result = result + skipped_items
   124→
   125→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   126→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   127→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   128→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   129→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   130→            if skip_entry:
   131→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   132→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   133→                if skip_reason:
   134→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   135→
   136→    # Filter to cluster if requested
   137→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   138→    if effective_cluster:
   139→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   140→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   141→        if cluster_member_ids:
   142→            result = [item for item in result if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids]
   143→
   144→    return result
   145→
   146→
   147→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   148→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   149→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   150→        return any(
   151→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   152→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   153→        )
   154→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   155→
   156→
   157→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   158→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   159→    try:
   160→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   161→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   162→        if meta:
   163→            return meta.action_type
   164→    except ImportError:
   165→        pass
   166→    return "manual_fix"
   167→
   168→
   169→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   170→
   171→
   172→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   173→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   174→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   175→    if not clusters:
   176→        return items
   177→
   178→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   179→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   180→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   181→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   182→            continue
   183→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   184→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   185→
   186→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   187→        return items
   188→
   189→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   190→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   191→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   192→
   193→    for item in items:
   194→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   195→        if cname:
   196→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   197→        else:
   198→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   199→
   200→    # Build cluster meta-items
   201→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   202→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   203→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   204→        if len(members) < 2:
   205→            result.extend(members)
   206→            continue
   207→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   208→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   209→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   210→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   211→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   212→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   213→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   214→            action_type = "reorganize"
   215→        else:
   216→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   217→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   218→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   219→                action_type = "refactor"
   220→
   221→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   222→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   223→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   224→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   225→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   226→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   227→        else:
   228→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   229→
   230→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   231→        if not primary_command:
   232→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   233→
   234→        meta_item: dict = {
   235→            "id": cname,
   236→            "kind": "cluster",
   237→            "action_type": action_type,
   238→            "summary": summary,
   239→            "members": members,
   240→            "member_count": len(members),
   241→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   242→            "cluster_name": cname,
   243→            "cluster_auto": True,
   244→            "confidence": "high",
   245→            "detector": detector,
   246→            "file": "",
   247→        }
   248→        result.append(meta_item)
   249→
   250→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   251→    return result
   252→
   253→
   254→def build_work_queue(
   255→    state: StateModel,
   256→    *,
   257→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   258→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   259→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   260→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   261→
   262→    status = resolved_options.status
   263→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   264→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   265→    try:
   266→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   267→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   268→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   269→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   270→
   271→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   272→        state,
   273→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   274→        status_filter=status,
   275→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   276→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   277→    )
   278→
   279→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   280→    if (
   281→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   282→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   283→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   284→    ):
   285→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   286→            state,
   287→            state.get("findings", {}),
   288→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   289→        )
   290→        for item in subjective_items:
   291→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   292→                all_items.append(item)
   293→
   294→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   295→
   296→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   297→    if resolved_options.plan:
   298→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   299→            all_items,
   300→            resolved_options.plan,
   301→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   302→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   303→        )
   304→
   305→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   306→    should_collapse = (
   307→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   308→        and resolved_options.plan
   309→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   310→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   311→    )
   312→    if should_collapse:
   313→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   314→
   315→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   316→
   317→    requested_tier = (
   318→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   319→    )
   320→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   321→    fallback_reason = None
   322→    filtered = all_items
   323→
   324→    if requested_tier is not None:
   325→        filtered = [
   326→            item
   327→            for item in all_items
   328→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   329→        ]
   330→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   331→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   332→            if chosen is not None:
   333→                selected_tier = chosen
   334→                filtered = [
   335→                    item
   336→                    for item in all_items
   337→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   338→                ]
   339→                fallback_reason = (
   340→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   341→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   342→                )
   343→        elif not filtered:
   344→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   345→
   346→    total = len(filtered)
   347→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   348→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   349→
   350→    if resolved_options.explain:
   351→        for item in filtered:
   352→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   353→
   354→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   355→    return {
   356→        "items": filtered,
   357→        "total": total,
   358→        "tier_counts": counts,
   359→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   360→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   361→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   362→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   363→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   364→    }
   365→
   366→
   367→__all__ = [
   368→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   369→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   370→    "WorkQueueResult",
   371→    "build_work_queue",
   372→    "group_queue_items",
   373→]
   374→
```

```
    18→
    19→ALL_STATUSES = set(finding_status_tokens(include_all=True))
    20→ATTEST_EXAMPLE = (
    21→    "I have actually [DESCRIBE THE CONCRETE CHANGE YOU MADE] "
    22→    "and I am not gaming the score by resolving without fixing."

    25→
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}
    30→
    31→
    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:
    33→    return status_filter == "all" or item_status == status_filter
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→
    44→
    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"
    47→
    48→
    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")
   105→
   106→
   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()

   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:

   122→    return {

   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }

   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""

   200→) -> list[dict]:

   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:

   253→        elif is_stale:
```

```
1→"""Canonical scorecard projection helpers shared across app/engine code."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import re
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import (
     8→    scorecard_dimension_rows,
     9→)
    10→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    11→
    12→_COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION: dict[str, str] = {
    13→    "abstraction leverage": "abstraction_fitness",
    14→    "indirection cost": "abstraction_fitness",
    15→    "interface honesty": "abstraction_fitness",
    16→}
    17→
    18→
    19→def dimension_cli_key(dimension_name: str) -> str:
    20→    """Map a display name to a review --dimensions CLI key."""
    21→    cleaned = dimension_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
    22→    lowered = cleaned.lower()
    23→    if lowered in _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION:
    24→        return _COMPONENT_TO_DIMENSION[lowered]
    25→    for key, display in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    26→        if display.lower() == lowered:
    27→            return key
    28→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "_", lowered).strip("_")
    29→
    30→
    31→def scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name: str, data: dict) -> list[str]:
    32→    """Map scorecard subjective display names to review --dimensions keys."""
    33→    if name in ("Elegance", "Elegance (combined)"):
    34→        components = (
    35→            data.get("detectors", {})
    36→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    37→            .get("components", [])
    38→        )
    39→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    40→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    41→        else:
    42→            keys = [
    43→                "high_level_elegance",
    44→                "mid_level_elegance",
    45→                "low_level_elegance",
    46→            ]
    47→    elif name in ("Abstraction fit", "Abstraction fit (combined)"):
    48→        components = (
    49→            data.get("detectors", {})
    50→            .get("subjective_assessment", {})
    51→            .get("components", [])
    52→        )
    53→        if isinstance(components, list) and components:
    54→            keys = [dimension_cli_key(component) for component in components]
    55→        else:
    56→            keys = ["abstraction_fitness"]
    57→    else:
    58→        keys = [dimension_cli_key(name)]
    59→
    60→    seen: set[str] = set()
    61→    ordered: list[str] = []
    62→    for key in keys:
    63→        if not key or key in seen:
    64→            continue
    65→        ordered.append(key)
    66→        seen.add(key)
    67→    return ordered
    68→
    69→
    70→def scorecard_subjective_entries(
    71→    state: dict,
    72→    *,
    73→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
    74→) -> list[dict]:
    75→    """Return scorecard-subjective entries with score/strict/placeholder metadata."""
    76→    rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
    77→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}
    78→    subjective_display_names = {display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    79→    subjective_display_names.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    80→
    81→    entries: list[dict] = []
    82→    for name, data in rows:
    83→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    84→        lowered_name = str(name).strip().lower()
    85→        is_subjective = (
    86→            "subjective_assessment" in detectors
    87→            or lowered_name in subjective_display_names
    88→        )
    89→        if not is_subjective:
    90→            continue
    91→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
    92→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
    93→        assessment_meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment", {})
    94→        placeholder = bool(
    95→            assessment_meta.get("placeholder")
    96→            or (
    97→                data.get("score", 0) == 0
    98→                and data.get("issues", 0) == 0
    99→                and data.get("checks", 0) == 0
   100→            )
   101→        )
   102→        [REDACTED]("dimension_key", "")
   103→        stale = bool(
   104→            dim_key
   105→            and isinstance(assessments.get(dim_key), dict)
   106→            and assessments[dim_key].get("needs_review_refresh")
   107→        )
   108→        entries.append(
   109→            {
   110→                "name": name,
   111→                "score": score,
   112→                "strict": strict,
   113→                "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   114→                "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   115→                "tier": int(data.get("tier", 4) or 4),
   116→                "placeholder": placeholder,
   117→                "stale": stale,
   118→                "dimension_key": dim_key,
   119→                "cli_keys": scorecard_dimension_cli_keys(name, data),
   120→            }
   121→        )
   122→    return entries
   123→
   124→
   125→def scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   126→    state: dict,
   127→    *,
   128→    dim_scores: dict | None = None,
   129→) -> list[dict]:
   130→    """Serialize scorecard rows for JSON/query outputs."""
   131→    subjective_by_name = {
   132→        entry["name"]: entry
   133→        for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   134→    }
   135→    payload: list[dict] = []
   136→    for name, data in scorecard_dimension_rows(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   137→        score = float(data.get("score", 0.0))
   138→        strict = float(data.get("strict", score))
   139→        subjective = name in subjective_by_name
   140→        entry = {
   141→            "name": name,
   142→            "score": score,
   143→            "strict": strict,
   144→            "checks": int(data.get("checks", 0) or 0),
   145→            "issues": int(data.get("issues", 0) or 0),
   146→            "tier": int(
   147→                data.get("tier", 4 if subjective else 3) or (4 if subjective else 3)
   148→            ),
   149→            "subjective": subjective,
   150→        }
   151→        if subjective:
   152→            sub = subjective_by_name[name]
   153→            entry["placeholder"] = bool(sub.get("placeholder"))
   154→            entry["cli_keys"] = list(sub.get("cli_keys", []))
   155→        payload.append(entry)
   156→    return payload
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "dimension_cli_key",
   161→    "scorecard_dimension_cli_keys",
   162→    "scorecard_dimension_rows",
   163→    "scorecard_dimensions_payload",
   164→    "scorecard_subjective_entries",
   165→]
   166→
```
```

1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 2
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"}
    12→
    13→
    14→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    15→    finding_id: Required[str]
    16→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    17→    reason: str | None
    18→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    19→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    20→    created_at: str
    21→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    22→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    23→
    24→
    25→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    26→    finding_id: Required[str]
    27→    description: str | None
    28→    note: str | None
    29→    cluster: str | None
    30→    created_at: str
    31→    updated_at: str
    32→
    33→
    34→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    35→    name: Required[str]
    36→    description: str | None
    37→    finding_ids: list[str]
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    41→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    42→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    43→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    44→
    45→
    46→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    47→    original_id: Required[str]
    48→    original_detector: str
    49→    original_file: str
    50→    original_summary: str
    51→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    52→    superseded_at: str
    53→    remapped_to: str | None
    54→    candidates: list[str]
    55→    note: str | None
    56→
    57→
    58→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    59→    version: Required[int]
    60→    created: Required[str]
    61→    updated: Required[str]
    62→    queue_order: list[str]
    63→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    64→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    65→    active_cluster: str | None
    66→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    67→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    68→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    69→
    70→
    71→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    72→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    73→    now = utc_now()
    74→    return {
    75→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    76→        "created": now,
    77→        "updated": now,
    78→        "queue_order": [],
    79→        "deferred": [],
    80→        "skipped": {},
    81→        "active_cluster": None,
    82→        "overrides": {},
    83→        "clusters": {},
    84→        "superseded": {},
    85→    }
    86→
    87→
    88→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    89→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
    90→
    91→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
    92→    """
    93→    defaults = empty_plan()
    94→    for key, value in defaults.items():
    95→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
    96→
    97→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    98→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    99→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   100→        plan["deferred"] = []
   101→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   102→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   103→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   104→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   105→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   106→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   107→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   108→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   109→
   110→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   111→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   112→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   113→    if deferred:
   114→        now = utc_now()
   115→        for fid in list(deferred):
   116→            if fid not in skipped:
   117→                skipped[fid] = {
   118→                    "finding_id": fid,
   119→                    "kind": "temporary",
   120→                    "reason": None,
   121→                    "note": None,
   122→                    "attestation": None,
   123→                    "created_at": now,
   124→                    "review_after": None,
   125→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   126→                }
   127→        deferred.clear()
   128→
   129→    # Normalize cluster fields
   130→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   131→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   132→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   133→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   134→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   135→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   136→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   137→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   138→
   139→
   140→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   141→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   142→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   143→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   144→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   145→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   146→
   147→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   148→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   149→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   150→    if overlap:
   151→        raise ValueError(
   152→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   153→        )
   154→
   155→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   156→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   157→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   158→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   159→            raise ValueError(
   160→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   161→            )
   162→
   163→
   164→__all__ = [
   165→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   166→    "Cluster",
   167→    "ItemOverride",
   168→    "PlanModel",
   169→    "SkipEntry",
   170→    "SupersededEntry",
   171→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   172→    "empty_plan",
   173→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   174→    "validate_plan",
   175→]
   176→
```

```
1→"""Tests for stale subjective dimension sync in the plan."""
     2→

    10→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11→# Helpers
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→
    14→def _plan_with_queue(*ids: str) -> dict:
    15→    plan = empty_plan()
    16→    plan["queue_order"] = list(ids)
    17→    return plan
    18→
    19→
    20→def _state_with_stale_dimensions(*dim_keys: str, score: float = 50.0) -> dict:
    21→    """Build a minimal state with stale subjective dimensions."""
    22→    dim_scores: dict = {}
    23→    assessments: dict = {}
    24→    for dim_key in dim_keys:
    25→        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
    26→            "score": score,
    27→            "strict": score,
    28→            "checks": 1,
    29→            "issues": 0,
    30→            "detectors": {
    31→                "subjective_assessment": {
    32→                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
    33→                    "placeholder": False,
    34→                }
    35→            },
    36→        }
    37→        assessments[dim_key] = {
    38→            "score": score,
    39→            "needs_review_refresh": True,
    40→            "refresh_reason": "mechanical_findings_changed",
    41→            "stale_since": "2025-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
    42→        }
    43→    return {
    44→        "findings": {},
    45→        "scan_count": 5,
    46→        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
    47→        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    48→    }
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# Injection: empty queue + stale dimensions
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def test_injects_when_queue_empty():
    56→    plan = _plan_with_queue()
    57→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
    58→
    59→    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
    60→    assert len(result.injected) == 2
    61→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]
    62→    assert "subjective::error_consistency" in plan["queue_order"]
    63→    assert result.changes == 2
    64→
    65→
    66→def test_no_injection_when_queue_has_real_items():
    67→    plan = _plan_with_queue("some_finding::file.py::abc123")
    68→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence")
    69→
    70→    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
    71→    assert result.injected == []
    72→    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in plan["queue_order"]
    73→

    97→
    98→def test_prunes_resolved_dimension_ids():

   100→        "subjective::design_coherence",

   118→

   122→
   123→
   124→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   125→# Full lifecycle: inject → refresh → prune → re-inject
   126→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   127→
   128→def test_full_lifecycle():
   129→    plan = _plan_with_queue()
   130→    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
   131→
   132→    # 1. Empty queue, stale dims → inject both
   133→    r1 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   134→    assert len(r1.injected) == 2
   135→    assert plan["queue_order"] == [
   136→        "subjective::design_coherence",
   137→        "subjective::error_consistency",
   138→    ]
   139→
   140→    # 2. User refreshes design_coherence (no longer stale)
   141→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   142→
   143→    r2 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   144→    assert r2.pruned == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   145→    # error_consistency still there — queue not empty, so no new injection
   146→    assert plan["queue_order"] == ["subjective::error_consistency"]
   147→    assert r2.injected == []
   148→
   149→    # 3. User refreshes error_consistency too → queue empties, nothing stale
   150→    state["subjective_assessments"]["error_consistency"]["needs_review_refresh"] = False
   151→
   152→    r3 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   153→    assert r3.pruned == ["subjective::error_consistency"]
   154→    assert plan["queue_order"] == []
   155→    assert r3.injected == []
   156→
   157→    # 4. New mechanical change makes design_coherence stale again
   158→    state["subjective_assessments"]["design_coherence"]["needs_review_refresh"] = True
   159→
   160→    r4 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
   161→    assert r4.injected == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   162→    assert plan["queue_order"] == ["subjective::design_coherence"]
   163→

   168→

   196→
```

```
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import (

    23→
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Helpers
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→def _finding(fid: str, detector: str = "unused", tier: int = 1,
    29→             file: str = "test.py", detail: dict | None = None) -> dict:
    30→    return {
    31→        "id": fid,
    32→        "detector": detector,
    33→        "file": file,
    34→        "tier": tier,
    35→        "confidence": "high",
    36→        "summary": f"Finding {fid}",
    37→        "status": "open",
    38→        "detail": detail or {},
    39→    }
    40→
    41→
    42→def _state_with(*findings: dict) -> dict:
    43→    fmap = {}
    44→    for f in findings:
    45→        fmap[f["id"]] = f
    46→    return {"findings": fmap, "scan_count": 5}
    47→
    48→
    49→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→
    53→def test_grouping_key_auto_fix():
    54→    from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
    55→    f = _finding("a", "unused")
    56→    meta = DETECTORS.get("unused")
    57→    key = _grouping_key(f, meta)

    64→    meta = DETECTORS.get("review")

   419→def test_generate_action_always_returns_something():
   420→    """Every detector/subtype combination must produce a non-None action."""
   421→    from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   422→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import _generate_action
   423→
   424→    # No metadata → fallback
   425→    assert _generate_action(None, None) == "review and fix each finding"
   426→
   427→    # Every registered detector, with and without subtype
   428→    for name, meta in DETECTORS.items():
   429→        result = _generate_action(meta, None)
   430→        assert result, f"_generate_action({name}, None) returned empty"
   431→
   432→        result_sub = _generate_action(meta, "some_subtype")
   433→        assert result_sub, f"_generate_action({name}, 'some_subtype') returned empty"
   434→
   435→
   436→def test_generate_action_strips_subtype_examples():
   437→    """Guidance with ' — ' should be stripped to the core verb for subtypes."""
   438→    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import _strip_guidance_examples
   439→
   440→    assert _strip_guidance_examples("fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains") == "fix code smells"
   441→    assert _strip_guidance_examples("fix dict key mismatches — dead writes are likely dead code") == "fix dict key mismatches"
   442→    # No dash → keep as-is
   443→    assert _strip_guidance_examples("review and fix each finding") == "review and fix each finding"
   444→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    92→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    93→        if finding_id in ids:
    94→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    95→
    96→    return True
    97→
    98→
    99→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   100→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   101→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   102→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   103→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   104→
   105→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   106→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   107→        try:
   108→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   109→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   110→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   111→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   112→                to_prune.append(fid)
   113→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   114→            to_prune.append(fid)
   115→
   116→    for fid in to_prune:
   117→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   118→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   119→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   120→
   121→    return to_prune
   122→
   123→
   124→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> ReconcileResult:
   128→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   129→
   130→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   131→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   132→    """
   133→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   134→    result = ReconcileResult()
   135→    now = utc_now()
   136→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   137→
   138→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   139→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   140→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   141→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   142→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   143→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   144→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   145→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   146→
   147→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   148→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   149→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   150→    referenced_ids = {
   151→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   152→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   153→    }
   154→
   155→    # Check each referenced ID
   156→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   157→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   158→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   159→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   160→                result.changes += 1
   161→
   162→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   163→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   164→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   165→
   166→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   167→    if resurfaced:
   168→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   169→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   170→
   171→    # Prune old superseded entries
   172→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   173→    result.pruned = pruned
   174→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   175→
   176→    return result
   177→
   178→
   179→__all__ = [
   180→    "ReconcileResult",
   181→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   182→]
   183→
```

## Implementation Plan: Initial Subjective Review Support

### Design Decisions (Answering Your 7 Questions)

**1. Should `sync_unscored_dimensions` be a separate function or extend `sync_stale_dimensions`?**

**Separate function.** The two operations have fundamentally different semantics:
- **Unscored**: unconditional injection to the FRONT of the queue. Runs every scan.
- **Stale**: conditional injection to the BACK, only when no objective items remain.

Mixing them into one function would create confusing branching and make the distinct behaviors harder to test. The existing `sync_stale_dimensions` has a clean contract (cleanup + conditional back-injection), and adding front-injection with different gating would muddy it. A separate `sync_unscored_dimensions` function in the same file (`stale_dimensions.py`) keeps them co-located since they share the same helpers (`scorecard_subjective_entries`, `SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX`, `slugify`) and data model.

**2. How should `auto_cluster_findings` distinguish unscored vs stale IDs?**

Both use the same `subjective::<slug>` ID format in `queue_order`, which is correct -- they ARE the same synthetic IDs, just in different states. The distinction is made at clustering time by checking the underlying state. The implementation:

- After collecting all `subjective::*` IDs from `queue_order`, partition them into two sets by re-querying `scorecard_subjective_entries`:
  - IDs where `entry.get("placeholder")` is True go into the `auto/initial-review` cluster.
  - IDs where `entry.get("stale")` is True go into the `auto/stale-review` cluster.
- Since requirement 4 says they are disjoint sets, there is no overlap concern.
- Use a new constant `_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"` and `_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"` (parallel to the existing `_STALE_KEY` / `_STALE_NAME`).

**3. Should we add a new `action_type` for the initial-review cluster, or reuse existing ones?**

**No new action_type.** The existing `action_type` values (`auto_fix`, `reorganize`, `refactor`, `manual_fix`) are detector-oriented. The initial-review cluster is a subjective-review batch -- it does not fit cleanly into any of those, but `manual_fix` is the correct semantic match (requires human judgment). The cluster rendering in `_render_cluster_item` already maps `manual_fix` to "Grouped task", which is acceptable for initial review.

However, we should add a special-case label in `_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS` lookup or in `_render_cluster_item` to detect the cluster name `auto/initial-review` and display "Initial subjective review" instead of the generic "Grouped task". This is a rendering-only concern and does not require a schema change.

**4. What is the right call order in `scan_workflow.py`?**

The current order in `merge_scan_results` (lines 427-478) is:

```
reconcile_plan_after_scan  ->  sync_stale_dimensions  ->  auto_cluster_findings
```

The new order should be:

```
reconcile_plan_after_scan  ->  sync_unscored_dimensions  ->  sync_stale_dimensions  ->  auto_cluster_findings
```

Why unscored before stale?
- Unscored sync prepends IDs to the FRONT of `queue_order`.
- Stale sync appends IDs to the BACK (when no objective items remain).
- Running unscored first means the cleanup pass of `sync_stale_dimensions` will correctly skip unscored IDs (they would not be in `_current_stale_ids` because placeholder=True dimensions are not stale -- requirement 4).
- Both must run before `auto_cluster_findings` because the cluster algorithm reads `queue_order` to find `subjective::*` IDs.

**5. What about `_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE` for initial review?**

**Yes, 1 unscored dimension should create a cluster.** The rationale: the initial review is a special onboarding step. Even a single unscored dimension represents actionable work the user needs to do before their scorecard is meaningful. The `_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2` threshold exists for objective findings (where singletons are better shown individually), but initial review is semantically a batch operation regardless of count.

Implementation: use `_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1` as a separate constant, applied only to the `auto/initial-review` cluster logic in `auto_cluster_findings`.

**6. How does queue ordering work end-to-end?**

Full flow after scan:

1. **`sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)`** -- Finds dimensions where `placeholder=True` via `scorecard_subjective_entries`. Generates `subjective::<slug>` IDs. Prepends them to `queue_order[0:0]` (front). Prunes any `subjective::*` IDs that are no longer unscored (user reviewed them). Returns `UnscoredDimensionSyncResult` with `injected` and `pruned` lists.

2. **`sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)`** -- Existing logic unchanged. Finds dimensions where `stale=True`. Since unscored IDs are NOT stale (disjoint, requirement 4), `_current_stale_ids` will not return them. The cleanup pass removes any `subjective::*` ID not in the stale set AND not in the unscored set (we need a small adjustment: the cleanup pass currently removes any `subjective::*` not in `stale_ids`, but after this change we must also exclude unscored IDs from pruning).

   **Wait -- this is a subtle interaction.** The existing cleanup in `sync_stale_dimensions` at line 68-74 removes `subjective::*` IDs that are NOT in `stale_ids`. If an unscored ID was just prepended by `sync_unscored_dimensions`, the stale cleanup would prune it because it is not in `stale_ids`.

   **Fix:** `sync_stale_dimensions` needs to know which IDs are unscored so it does not prune them. Options:
   - (A) Pass the unscored set explicitly.
   - (B) Have `sync_stale_dimensions` also compute the unscored set and exclude those from pruning.
   - (C) Refactor to have one function that computes both sets and handles cleanup holistically.

   **Best approach: (B)**. Have `sync_stale_dimensions` call a helper `_current_unscored_ids(state)` and exclude those from the prune set. This keeps the two sync functions independent (no parameter coupling) while preventing the prune-collision. Since `_current_unscored_ids` is cheap (same `scorecard_subjective_entries` call, just filtering on `placeholder` instead of `stale`), the duplication is minimal.

3. **`auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)`** -- Partitions `subjective::*` IDs from `queue_order` into unscored vs stale by checking state. Creates `auto/initial-review` cluster for unscored (min size 1), `auto/stale-review` for stale (min size 2).

4. **`_apply_plan_order(items, plan)`** in `core.py` -- At `build_work_queue` time: items in `queue_order` appear first. Since unscored IDs are at the front of `queue_order`, they get position 1, 2, etc.

5. **`_collapse_clusters(items, plan)`** -- Replaces individual subjective items belonging to `auto/initial-review` with a single cluster meta-item. The sort key for clusters is `(0, action_priority, ...)`, so all clusters sort before all individual findings.

6. **Rendering in `next`** -- `_render_cluster_item` displays the cluster with its label. For `auto/initial-review`, we override the label to "Initial subjective review" instead of "Grouped task".

**7. Edge cases:**

- **Partial reviews**: If the user reviews 2 of 5 unscored dimensions, the next scan's `sync_unscored_dimensions` will only inject the remaining 3 (those still `placeholder=True`). The reviewed ones get pruned.
- **Re-scans**: Each scan is idempotent. `sync_unscored_dimensions` prunes already-reviewed IDs and only injects those still unscored. No duplicate injection because existing IDs are checked before prepending.
- **Language with no subjective dims**: `scorecard_subjective_entries` returns an empty list. Both sync functions return `changes=0`. No cluster created. No-op.
- **First scan ever (no prior state)**: All subjective dimensions start as `placeholder=True`. All get injected at front. This is the primary use case.
- **`--reset-subjective` flag**: This explicitly sets `placeholder=True` on all dimensions (see `_reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset` at line 176 of `scan_workflow.py`). After reset, all dims would be unscored again, which is correct -- the user asked for a fresh start.
- **Stale + unscored collision (should not happen by requirement 4)**: A dimension cannot be both `placeholder=True` and `stale=True` simultaneously. The `stale` field requires `needs_review_refresh=True` on the assessment, which is only set for previously-scored dimensions. `placeholder=True` means never scored. So they are truly disjoint by data model.
- **User manually moves a `subjective::*` ID to a different queue position or skips it**: The sync functions respect existing IDs (they check `existing = set(order)` before inserting). If the ID is already in queue_order, it will not be re-added. If it is in `skipped`, it stays skipped and is not re-injected. This is correct -- user intent overrides automatic placement.

#### File 1: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`

**Add `_current_unscored_ids` function** (parallel to `_current_stale_ids`):

```python
def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder)."""
    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    )

    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    if not dim_scores:
        return set()

    unscored: set[str] = set()
    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
        if not entry.get("placeholder"):
            continue
        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
        if dim_key:
            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    return unscored
```

**Add `UnscoredDimensionSyncResult` dataclass** (parallel to `StaleDimensionSyncResult`):

```python
@dataclass
class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def changes(self) -> int:
        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
```

**Add `sync_unscored_dimensions` function**:

```python
def sync_unscored_dimensions(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    """Sync unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions into the plan queue.

    1. Remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from queue_order that are no longer unscored.
    2. Prepend any currently-unscored dimension IDs to the FRONT of queue_order.
    
    Unlike stale sync, injection is unconditional (not gated on queue emptiness).
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)  # Need this to avoid pruning stale IDs
    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]

    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored AND not stale
    # (Don't prune stale IDs here -- that's sync_stale_dimensions' job)
    to_remove: list[str] = [
        fid for fid in order
        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
        and fid not in unscored_ids
        and fid not in stale_ids
    ]
    for fid in to_remove:
        order.remove(fid)
        result.pruned.append(fid)

    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front of queue
    existing = set(order)
    to_inject = sorted(uid for uid in unscored_ids if uid not in existing)
    for uid in reversed(to_inject):  # reversed so first alphabetically ends up at index 0
        order.insert(0, uid)
        result.injected.append(uid)

    return result
```

**Add unscored cluster logic** in `auto_cluster_findings`, immediately after the stale cluster block (line 337-383). The new block:

```python
# --- Unscored (initial review) subjective dimension cluster ---------------
# Partition subjective IDs into unscored vs stale using state
from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import _current_unscored_ids

unscored_state_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
    fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
    if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) and fid in unscored_state_ids
)

if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
    active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
    cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
    description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimension{'s' if len(unscored_queue_ids) != 1 else ''}"
    action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"

    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY)
    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
        old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
        new_ids = set(unscored_queue_ids)
        if old_ids != new_ids or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
            cluster["finding_ids"] = unscored_queue_ids
            cluster["description"] = description
            cluster["action"] = action
            cluster["updated_at"] = now
            changes += 1
    else:
        clusters[_UNSCORED_NAME] = {
            "name": _UNSCORED_NAME,
            "description": description,
            "finding_ids": unscored_queue_ids,
            "created_at": now,
            "updated_at": now,
            "auto": True,
            "cluster_key": _UNSCORED_KEY,
            "action": action,
            "user_modified": False,
        }
        existing_by_key[_UNSCORED_KEY] = _UNSCORED_NAME
        changes += 1

    # Track cluster membership in overrides
    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    current_name = existing_by_key.get(_UNSCORED_KEY, _UNSCORED_NAME)
    for fid in unscored_queue_ids:
        if fid not in overrides:
            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
```

```python
def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
    
    # Special labels for subjective review clusters
    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
    else:
        action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
```

```python
# Helper for unscored state
def _state_with_unscored_dimensions(*dim_keys: str) -> dict:
    """Build a minimal state with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions."""
    dim_scores: dict = {}
    assessments: dict = {}
    for dim_key in dim_keys:
        dim_scores[dim_key] = {
            "score": 0.0,
            "strict": 0.0,
            "checks": 0,
            "issues": 0,
            "detectors": {
                "subjective_assessment": {
                    "dimension_key": dim_key,
                    "placeholder": True,
                }
            },
        }
        assessments[dim_key] = {
            "score": 0.0,
            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
            "placeholder": True,
        }
    return {
        "findings": {},
        "scan_count": 1,
        "dimension_scores": dim_scores,
        "subjective_assessments": assessments,
    }

def test_unscored_injected_at_front():
    plan = _plan_with_queue("some_finding::file.py::abc123")
    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence", "error_consistency")
    
    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
    assert len(result.injected) == 2
    # Unscored IDs at FRONT, existing finding at back
    assert plan["queue_order"][-1] == "some_finding::file.py::abc123"
    assert plan["queue_order"][0].startswith("subjective::")
    assert plan["queue_order"][1].startswith("subjective::")

def test_unscored_injection_unconditional():
    """Unlike stale, unscored injects even when objective items exist."""
    plan = _plan_with_queue("real_finding::x.py::abc")
    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence")
    
    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
    assert len(result.injected) == 1
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]

def test_unscored_pruned_after_review():
    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")
    # State no longer has placeholder=True for design_coherence
    state = _state_with_stale_dimensions()  # empty stale set
    state["dimension_scores"] = {}  # no unscored either
    
    result = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in result.pruned

def test_stale_sync_does_not_prune_unscored_ids():
    """sync_stale_dimensions must not prune IDs that are unscored."""
    plan = _plan_with_queue("subjective::design_coherence")
    state = _state_with_unscored_dimensions("design_coherence")
    
    result = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" not in result.pruned
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in plan["queue_order"]

def test_unscored_and_stale_are_disjoint():
    """A dimension cannot be both unscored and stale."""
    plan = _plan_with_queue()
    # Build state with one unscored AND one stale
    dim_scores = {}
    assessments = {}
    # Unscored dimension
    dim_scores["design_coherence"] = {
        "score": 0.0, "strict": 0.0, "checks": 0, "issues": 0,
        "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"[REDACTED], "placeholder": True}},
    }
    assessments["design_coherence"] = {"score": 0.0, "placeholder": True}
    # Stale dimension
    dim_scores["error_consistency"] = {
        "score": 50.0, "strict": 50.0, "checks": 1, "issues": 0,
        "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"[REDACTED], "placeholder": False}},
    }
    assessments["error_consistency"] = {"score": 50.0, "needs_review_refresh": True}
    state = {"findings": {}, "scan_count": 5, "dimension_scores": dim_scores, "subjective_assessments": assessments}
    
    # Run both syncs in correct order
    r1 = sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)
    r2 = sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
    
    # design_coherence should be at front (unscored), error_consistency should not be injected (has_real_items is False BUT only subjective in queue)
    assert "subjective::design_coherence" in r1.injected
    # Note: stale injection only happens when no real items exist, and "subjective::design_coherence" is not a "real" item
```

```python
def test_initial_review_cluster_created():
    """Unscored dims in queue create an auto/initial-review cluster."""
    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
    
    plan = empty_plan()
    plan["queue_order"] = ["subjective::design_coherence", "subjective::error_consistency"]
    
    # State with placeholder=True (unscored)
    state = {
        "findings": {},
        "scan_count": 1,
        "dimension_scores": {
            "design_coherence": {"score": 0, "strict": 0, "checks": 0, "issues": 0,
                "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"[REDACTED], "placeholder": True}}},
            "error_consistency": {"score": 0, "strict": 0, "checks": 0, "issues": 0,
                "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"[REDACTED], "placeholder": True}}},
        },
        "subjective_assessments": {
            "design_coherence": {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
            "error_consistency": {"score": 0, "placeholder": True},
        },
    }
    
    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
    assert changes >= 1
    assert "auto/initial-review" in plan["clusters"]
    cluster = plan["clusters"]["auto/initial-review"]
    assert cluster["auto"] is True
    assert cluster["cluster_key"] == "subjective::unscored"
    assert "Initial review" in cluster["description"]

def test_single_unscored_dim_creates_cluster():
    """Even 1 unscored dim creates a cluster (unlike stale which needs 2)."""
    from desloppify.engine._plan.auto_cluster import auto_cluster_findings
    
    plan = empty_plan()
    plan["queue_order"] = ["subjective::design_coherence"]
    state = {
        "findings": {},
        "scan_count": 1,
        "dimension_scores": {
            "design_coherence": {"score": 0, "strict": 0, "checks": 0, "issues": 0,
                "detectors": {"subjective_assessment": {"[REDACTED], "placeholder": True}}},
        },
        "subjective_assessments": {"design_coherence": {"score": 0, "placeholder": True}},
    }
    
    changes = auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
    assert "auto/initial-review" in plan["clusters"]
```

---

### Summary of Call Sequence After Implementation

```
merge_scan_results
  |
  +-- reconcile_plan_after_scan(plan, state)
  |     Removes dead finding IDs from plan. Skips subjective:: IDs.
  |
  +-- sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, state)         # NEW
  |     Prunes reviewed dims. Prepends unscored dims to queue FRONT.
  |
  +-- sync_stale_dimensions(plan, state)
  |     Prunes refreshed stale dims (excluding unscored).
  |     Appends stale dims to queue BACK (only if no objective items).
  |
  +-- auto_cluster_findings(plan, state)
        Partitions subjective::* IDs into unscored vs stale.
        Creates auto/initial-review (min 1) and auto/stale-review (min 2).
        Creates auto-clusters for objective findings.
```
